The roadmap's words already rewrite only when the training changes (`source_hash`), and a coach's wording always wins — why: on Nov 18 2026 the coach found notes generic, repetitive and unnecessary: 340 generated cards said only "Progress: Double progression.", week 2+ top sets read "Week 1: …", cards explained the model's choices ("New addition for 3D shoulder look"), Devin's walk said "Your nutrition plan will adjust this when it's made." a week after it had (20270409100000 took it off his two plans), and 126 of the 211 day notes clients could see were word for word last week's (Jarrett's 803-character note on all 96 of his days).
- The top of the logger, above the first exercise, says each thing once and quietly (Nov 18 2026, logger-top.tsx): the title is the workout's name, the one they tapped on Workouts (`loggerTitle`: subtitle, else title, "Day 1" only when it has none), one muted line says where it sits (`loggerMeta`: "Day 1 · General Strength · Week 3", the block by its own name), and the progress pill (ring = sets logged, "3/9" = exercises, the cardio's pulse line, the clock) rides on the back button's row (`PageHeader actions`); a workout opened on another day adds one muted line under it (`ScheduledDayLine`: "Scheduled for tomorrow. You can still do it today, and its day stays the same.", `scheduledDayWords`), never an amber card. Then one row that never wraps (`data-logger-controls`): Warm-up on the left only while there's a warm-up with movements to open (`WarmupButton hideWhenEmpty` → `warmupHasContent`, the same query the sheet reads; never on a finished workout), Log faster as a ? and List / Focus on the right (the toggle drops its icons under 360px). Coach notes are a compact block (`DayCoachNotes`: no border, no caps label, the first paragraph to three lines, the rest and anything longer behind More, opening in place; never the brand red). The defaults in `warmup_protocols` (General, Powerlifting) are readable only by staff (RLS `warmup_protocols_client_read_assigned` lets a client read only an assigned one), so until that changes a client's Warm-up stays hidden and a coach in View as client still sees it — why: on Nov 18 2026 the coach found the top of the logger cluttered: the List / Focus switch wrapped onto a line of its own beside Warm-Up and Log faster, the cardio heart read as a favourite button, the title was a bare "Day 1", the notes sat in a bordered card under a caps label with a red Read more, the finished card took three rows, an amber card repeated the schedule, and Warm-Up opened an empty full screen for every client (no client can read the default warm-ups).
- The Workouts day card has one main button (Start, Continue, Finish review or View; a missed workout leads with Start) plus Preview; Reschedule, Change status and Edit live in its ⋯, once each. In the logger a finished workout shows one card at the top (`CompletedWorkoutActions`: "Completed" over when the session ran as one tappable line, `status` → `SessionTimeRow lead`, then View score and the review side by side), the list view's clock rides in the progress pill (`WorkoutTimer variant="inline"`), Training help is in each card's ⋯ (no header ?; Focus's ? is FocusTips), one tap on "+ Add set" adds a working set with "+ Warm-up" beside it where warm-ups are allowed, and the card has no order number (Focus's "Exercise N of M" says it) and its role is plain words (`purposeLabelText`) — why: the card had five buttons, "Start Early" opened the same page as View, a finished workout said "completed" twice, and adding a set took two taps.
- The coach's daily screens say things once: the dashboard has no quick-action grid (each action has its page), hides tiles at zero and shows one "All caught up" line when all are; it is change requests, the 7-day schedule, check-ins to review (the next rule), leads waiting, upcoming meets, the tiles and Training today as one row ("3 of 8 done" + faces) that opens its list, gone when nobody trains, and nothing else: no Wins (records are Crew's props queue and Wednesday Wins) and no Training to check (missed workouts and inactivity are the Clients list's attention sort and filters, pain is Messages' red line; 2 pain flags ever), and `admin_dashboard_overview` builds only today's training (20270122090000_dashboard_overview_training_only.sql: the Wins part walked every client's rep history, ~600ms on each 60s refresh, now ~11ms); a client's Summary is Calendar sync, App activity and one Missing card (login problems + missing fields, only when something's missing), never repeating the header, and the client profile's header (the page and the Clients overlay alike) is Message, View as client (the eye alone on a phone, never hidden) and one ⋯ (`moreMenu`: Today's workout, Training program, Program history, booking, lifecycle), entering client view only through `useEnterClientPov`; on Forms the in-app agreement comes first and the hand-set record and SignNow fold under "Older agreements", shown only when the client has something in them. Communication opens on Chats and Calendar on the board unless `?tab=` says otherwise (never the last tab used), with no second titles inside their tabs; the coach's chat header is Done, Call and Notes plus ⋯ (View as client, Send a text, …), all neutral, and the client's photo opens their profile — why: the coach saw the same action two to four times per screen and pages reopening on whatever tab was used last, and on a phone the profile opened from Clients had no View as client at all (hidden below `sm`, missing from its ⋯).
- What's waiting on the coach's review is one rule, review-queue.ts `waitingForReview` (pure, tested): a check-in or form that isn't reviewed (its own mark; a staff reply sets it, `tg_staff_reply_closes_reviews`), isn't superseded, came in within `TO_REVIEW_DAYS` (30) and is the client's newest of its type (a newer one, reviewed or not, retires the older, so reviewing the newest never brings an older one back). The dashboard's Check-ins to review card (`CheckinsToReviewCard`, `listFormSubmissionsFn({ scope: "review" })`, hidden at zero, longest waiting first), Forms → Submissions' To review (`toReviewKeys`, `?tab=submissions&status=to_review` opens on it) and the Messages inbox's Forms to review (`latestToReview`) all read it, so their counts agree. A submission opens in one sheet wherever it's opened (`SubmissionSheet`, submission-review.tsx: the answers, Open in chat, Mark reviewed; a form's responses viewer has the same), and Open in chat lands on the message it sits on, never just the conversation: `findSubmissionMessage` (submission-message.ts: a check-in's `checkin_submission` bubble, else its request; a form's newest `form_request` card sent at or before it, `pickRequestMessage`; jsonb containment sent as JSON text) → `/admin/messages?client=…&msg=…`, and the thread (`MessageThread focusMessageId`) loads back to it in one read when it's older than the loaded page (`listMessagesBetween`), centres it, highlights it and stops following the bottom (a check-in folded into its history row lands on that row) — why: on Nov 19 2026 the dashboard's "Check-ins to review" tile opened the Clients list filtered to those clients, so reviewing one took a client, a tab and a scroll through the chat, and the three counts disagreed: the tile counted clients with any unreviewed check-in ever, Forms counted every unreviewed one in 30 days, and Messages the newest per type within 14 days that no staff message (automated ones included) had followed.
- The staff menu is one registry (`internal-nav.ts` → `buildInternalNavCollapsed`), one row per page: Dashboard · Tasks · Messages · Clients · Crew (Feed, League, Athlete Records) · Calendar · Coaching (Forms, Programs) · Sales · Insights (App & website, Usage & spend) · Membership (Overview, Members) · Team · Settings. An address that only redirects to a tab of a listed page stays as `searchOnly` (More's search, the sitemap and the floating-bar picker find it, it lights its row and its badge counts there), and every page stays in the ⌘K palette (`admin-route-registry.ts`); no route is deleted for leaving the menu. `admin-nav.ts` holds only the client portal's menus. Exactly one row is lit (the deepest match), desktop has one search (the sidebar's ⌘K), Sign out lives only in the avatar menu, page headers carry no Crew pill (Crew's badge is on its row and tab) (the Coaching Hub is gone, the next rule). The phone More sheet never lists a page that's already a bottom-bar tab (its search still finds it) and has no Appearance row (light / dark is the top bar's switch) — why: 88 menu entries, a third of them redirects into tabs of pages already listed, three lists to keep in sync, Dashboard lit beside the page you were on, and five of the client's 17 More rows repeated the tab bar.
- There is no Coaching Hub (Nov 19 2026): `/admin/coaching` only redirects, each tab to where the coach already works: Training Intel and Management → Clients sorted by what needs attention (`/admin/clients?sort=attention`: missed workouts, inactive, reviews, programs ending; pain flags are Messages' red line), Progress → Forms → Submissions on progress (`?kind=progress`), Schedules → Forms → More → Check-in schedules (`?tab=schedules`, `AdminCoachingSchedules`), the old check-in queue → Submissions; `/admin/training-intelligence` and `/admin/client-action-requests` land on Clients too. Training Intel's "Event approaching" is the dashboard's Upcoming meets card (`MeetReadinessCard`: every coaching client, a coach's or admin's own athlete account included, with a meet on an Active / Planned meet prep or their approved competition plan within `UPCOMING_MEET_DAYS` (112, 16 weeks: a full peak block; 56 hid the coach's own Feb 8 2027 Nationals, 81 days out and already being peaked for; meet-board.ts `athleteMeets`, one per date, the prep's name and the plan's "not registered"), their later meets in the window under it, with or without a checklist, "send it from their chat" inside 28 days) and its Focus usage card is Workspace Settings'. The tables (`coach_followups`, `coach_intel_reviews`, `coach_pain_flags`) stay as history, and a client's Training Intel card on their program page (`ClientTrainingIntelCard`) stays — why: on Nov 19 2026 the coach asked whether the hub still had a purpose: follow-ups had never been used (0 ever), Training Intel's reviews were last marked Sep 30, the Progress queue held 53 items nobody had ever reviewed there, Management repeated the Clients list, and every signal it showed already lived somewhere the coach looks every day.
- Who a form is out with is read one way everywhere (form-response-status.ts, pure, tested; the same rule as `nf_form_library_stats()`, 20270415090000_form_responses_status.sql): a person is anyone it was asked of (an `nf_assignments` row, asked = its `updated_at`) or who sent or started it, and they have one state about the latest request (`personState`: answered = a response at or after the last ask, a minute's grace, or never asked; in progress = a draft touched since, the row or any answer on it (`answers_touched_at`: saving an answer never moves the submission's own row); waiting = asked and nothing since, `askedAgain` when they'd answered before; a form they keep updating that they've sent is asked again only by a fresh copy, `settings.new_copy_since`, never a nudge, `effectiveAsk`, 20270416090000_form_status_keeps_updating.sql). An archived client counts only once they've answered (`counted`; one asked again since shows as Archived for their past answers, counted apart, so every list adds up), so answered + in progress + waiting = sent, always; the viewer never opens another athlete in place of one it can't find, and a cleared number box is no answer (`formAnswerText`), never a 0; `responses` and `to_review` (unreviewed, last 30 days) count every response. `nf_assignments.client_id` references `clients` (an embed `client:client_id(...)` fails without it). A form's responses open in one viewer (`FormResponsesSheet` / `FormResponsesView`, form-responses.tsx) from the library's Responses, the editor's Responses tab, a chat card's View full response, a client's Forms card (Open in responses, on that client, `clientId`; the card reads the same rule) and Submissions: Summary (form-response-summary.ts: each question once, from each person's latest response; choices counted in the form's own option order, a rating's or number's average and spread, a checklist's still-needed first, written answers by who and when), Individual (one person at a time with ‹ › and a picker, their earlier responses as dates, Mark reviewed, Message, ⋯ Open profile / Fix an answer (`NativeAnswers`), a competition plan's Approve) and Table (everyone's latest response, filtered by state and sorted, a row opening Individual). The library is one list (`FormRow`: the title, one meta line, a status line on the same counts, Responses with its count, and ⋯ for share, preview, settings, duplicate and archive; New form and a ⋯ with Select forms / Sync from Fillout at the top; a To review chip only while a form has an unreviewed response). The editor is full screen on a phone with Questions first. A sent form's chat card is the form, when it came in, Reviewed / To review, the one key thing (a competition plan's approval, a Nutrition Update's AI plan line, else the first 3 answers) and one View full response — why: on Nov 19 2026 the coach found the editor's Responses tab saying "Nobody's been sent this form yet" while the library listed answers: the missing key made the read fail, and the error read as empty. The same failure left "Shared with" unticked, the meet board without sent checklists and the meet reminders reaching nobody. The library's "Sent to 14 · 8 answered · 7 waiting" counted on three different rules. He asked for Google Forms' way of reading responses, and chat cards that don't stack every answer.
- A client's payment on Coaching setup is one switch, "No payment needed" (`clients.payment_status` = 'Complimentary' / 'Not Sent', the same as the Clients list's ⋯), never a hand-set status: what a client owes comes from their sales (`admin_clients_directory` reads `payment_status` only for 'Complimentary'). Asking for a PT session is the coaching layer (`useEntitlement("pt_sessions")` on Home's calendar card and the Calendar page): a JF member books through an open booking type instead. The old check-in queue is gone (`/admin/coaching?tab=check-ins` opens Forms → Submissions; a client's manual reviews stay on their Forms tab), a client page's `?tab=check-ins` / `?tab=forms` open Forms (`LEGACY_WORKSPACE_TABS`), and "Coming soon" pages are gone (their addresses redirect, below). An off `Switch` is `bg-muted-foreground/35`, never the near-white `input` token — why: 15 of 16 coaching clients sat on the dropdown's "Not Sent" and it had no "Complimentary", so saving a comp client's setup could clear their exemption; a member's request was answered in a coach chat members can't open; the old queue had no use since August; check-in pushes landed on Summary; and an off switch was invisible on a white card.
- The coaching application (/coaching/apply, `QuestionFlow`) is one question per screen in `APPLY_STEPS` order (name, goal, result, start, email, phone, Instagram) under a progress bar and "Question N of 7"; choices move on by themselves, and Next always answers: when an answer won't do it says what to fix (`stepError`), never a button greyed out until the whole form is valid. apply-form.ts is the one set of rules for the page and the server (`publicCoachingApplicationSchema`): any phone with 7 to 15 digits (`phoneOk`), an @handle, a bare handle or a pasted profile link for Instagram (`instagramHandle`), and attribution (page URL, referrer, `?from=`) trimmed with `clip`, never refused. Answers stay on the phone (`jf_apply_v2`, 7 days) and, once there's an email or phone, are saved as an unfinished application (`saveApplicationDraft` → `coaching_application_drafts`, server-written, admin read, 20270118090000_coaching_application_drafts.sql) that Submit marks done (`draft_id`); Forms → Applications shows "Started, didn't finish" (`listApplicationDrafts`: not ones who applied on another try, nor ones set aside) — why: Devin filled in the old one-page form from Instagram on Nov 1 and nothing reached the app (no application, lead or email), its Apply button stayed greyed out with only a small "still needed" line when a field didn't pass, and nobody could tell he'd tried until he messaged.
- Progress photos and videos are taken the same way every time: each empty pose tile shows how to stand for it (`PoseFigure`, pose-guide.tsx: front, left side, back, right side, the right the left mirrored; its cue from `POSE_CUES`; a single video's tile says the turn, `SINGLE_VIDEO_CUE`), and the photo and video dialogs' "How to take them" (`PoseGuideButton` → `PoseGuideContent`) shows the setup drawn (phone at belly-button height, 6 to 8 feet away) and written (`PHOTO_SETUP`: first thing in the morning, light from in front, upright phone, head to feet in the frame, whatever they're comfortable in that shows their shape (a sports bra and shorts, a swimsuit or underwear; their call, worded so it reads right for women too, with the photos private to them and their coach) and the same each time, relaxed), the framing, each pose (`POSE_DETAIL`) and, for videos, the quarter-turn take (`VIDEO_STEPS`); the words live in progress-pose-guide.ts and the figures are drawn in the theme's own colours, never photos of a person — why: the tiles were a camera icon and "Tap to add photo" with nothing on how to take them, so check-ins shot from different heights, distances and light couldn't be compared.
- A client's bodyweight reads the same for the coach as for the client: weigh-ins are `progress_bodyweight` (by login, written only through `save_progress_bodyweight` / `delete_progress_bodyweight`, one per day), and `progress_metrics` is the coach-entered history from before it (nothing written since Aug 19 2026; still where a client with no login keeps a coach's entries). Anything that shows a client's bodyweight reads both, one entry a day with the app's winning (`mergeBodyweightSeries`; for a coach, `loadClientBodyweight`, client-bodyweight.ts, read whole, page by page), never `progress_metrics` alone. The coach's Progress tab panel (`ProgressMetricsPanel`, titled Bodyweight) is Latest (its day), 7-day average, Weekly change (that average against the 7 days before, `bodyweightTiles`; none unless both weeks have a weigh-in) and days logged this week, then the trend, the goal and the recent weigh-ins, each editable where it lives (`BodyweightEntryDialog`: a new one with a login goes through `logBodyweight`, so the client sees it), and a change refreshes the client's screens too (`clientBodyweightRefreshKeys`); the analytics dashboard under it leaves its own bodyweight card out (`hideBodyweight`). A coach's access to a client's progress (weigh-ins, measurements, check-in schedules, progress photos / videos and reviews) is the coach's sets, `coached_client_ids()` / `coached_client_user_ids()` / `coached_progress_submission_ids()` (20270222090000_coach_reads_client_bodyweight.sql), and the save / delete RPCs let the assigned coach through; never `clients.assigned_coach_id` compared with the login (it's a `coaches` row id) — why: Marc's Progress tab said 162.5 lb on Aug 17 and "No bodyweight entries in this range" while he'd weighed in almost every morning since (156.6 lb on Nov 9), because the panel read only the table nothing writes any more, and every coach rule on the progress tables compared a coach record's id with the login, so a coach who isn't an admin would have seen none of it.
- A coach's notes on a client are one list, `client_file_notes` (admins and the client's own coach only: RLS through `coached_client_ids()`, never the client; a nutrition note is one of them with `topic = 'nutrition'`, tagged "Nutrition" in the list and shown on the Nutrition tab's Food & diet card), shown where the coach looks: first on the client's Summary (`ClientFileNotes limit={3}`: pinned, then newest, "Show all N" in place, Add note), at the top of Notes in their chat above what they've sent (`ClientNotesSheet coachNotes`, `bare`; it's staff-only, the workout note card opens it only for staff) and in full with the Archive on More → Coach notes. A Quick Note saved to a client (Tasks) is synced into it both ways (`pushQuickNote` / `fetchLinkedCopies`, client-file-notes.ts) and deleting the Quick Note only archives the client's copy. The card's wrapper is a stable element type (`Wrap`), never a component made in render (it would remount the composer on every keystroke) — why: on Nov 11 2026 the coach's Quick Notes on Colten (nose surgery Nov 30, two weeks off; a possible move) were saved on his file but only under More → Coach notes, while his profile opens on Summary and the chat's Notes showed only what Colten had sent.
- Screens carry only what people use, the rest one tap away (round 2, from 30 days of production use: sets 3,109, workouts 175, bodyweight 165, client messages 256 against measurements 2 ever, wearables 0 ever, saves 0 ever). Workouts: no legend under the week strip (Appointments is in the header ⋯), no 0% ring before a set is logged, Today's readiness is one row that opens its breakdown in a sheet (`ReadinessSheet`: factor detail inside it, Back steps out one level), and the month is one line ("12 workouts · 3 PRs · 4-week streak", nothing at zero) with weight lifted / strongest lift / most trained under More; on the athlete's own Workouts both show only once they hold something (`hideWhenEmpty`: no "finish a few workouts to unlock it" or "your numbers start with your first workout" card; analytics stays in ⋯). Home's Progress card has no Measurements row and the Progress page's top row is Log weight · Add photos · Add video (measurements: a quiet link and their own tab); its Photos / Videos / Body composition cards show once they hold something. The client's Account (`/portal/account`) is a short settings list: photo, name and email, then grouped rows that open in place (`Accordion type="multiple"`, `SettingsSection`) with their status on the right and no section-jump bar; a link opens its section through `SECTION_ALIASES` (`#legal` from `LegalNotice` plus the old anchors; a new deep link adds its alias), the cards in it render `bare`, Goals & Setup and Coaching setup are a coaching client's only (`useEntitlement("goals_setup")` / tier, behind the tier skeleton), and Devices & recovery lists only what can connect on this device (`connectableProviders`: the phone's own health store inside the app, Oura once set up, never "coming soon"), its row shown only when something is connected or can connect. The coach's Tasks page is "Tasks" with no tagline, and in-page switches (Clients / Members / Team, My tasks / Team, Coaching / + Google) and checkboxes are neutral — why: the coach found every screen cluttered, Account was 6,685px on a phone with every form open, the Clients list showed up to ten pills per client, and a Profile notes box on Account never saved anywhere (there is no `clients.notes` column), so it was removed.
- The top of every screen on an iPhone / iPad: iOS 26 draws its own blur over whatever web content sits under the status bar (home-screen apps with `black-translucent` + `viewport-fit=cover`, and the native app's web view) unless a `position: fixed` element at least 90% wide, more than 10px deep and with an opaque background colour is at the top edge from the first frame, which it then uses as the solid colour there. That element is `.ios-status-band` (styles.css), the first thing in `<body>` in `__root.tsx`: full width, `max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-top))` deep, `var(--background)` (the View as client banner's tint while it holds the notch), `background-clip: text` so it's read and never painted, topmost, `pointer-events: none`; top bars stay opaque with no `backdrop-filter` (Safari reads a blur as several colours); the one exception is Crew's bar over the paged feed, see-through below its solid black notch, with the strip black there (`html.feed-paging .ios-status-band`). The shell's phone bar is itself a fixed bar at that edge (`.shell-bar`: `fixed`, opaque, full width, the notch inside its padding, `z-40`), its room held in the page by `data-shell-bar-room` (49px + the notch), sliding away while you read down like Crew's (`useBarHidden`) and hidden while typing (`data-keyboard-open`). The iPhone home-screen app (`navigator.standalone`, marked `data-ios-web-app` on `<html>` by `IOS_WEB_APP_BOOT_SCRIPT` before the first paint, ios-edge.ts) has a solid status bar (`apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style` "default", never "black-translucent"): the app starts right under the clock with nothing under the status bar for iOS to blur, in the page's own colour (`THEME_COLOR` = each theme's `--background`, painted from the first frame by `THEME_COLOR_SCRIPT` after the head's tags; black over Crew's paged feed, `setFeedPaging`), and the shell fills the window's measured height (`--app-vh`), since iOS's 100vh counts the status bar there (WebKit 316008). iOS reads that setting only when the app is added to the Home Screen, so an app added before it still draws under a transparent status bar, where from iOS 26.1 iOS blurs about 40pt under the notch over any web content, fixed opaque bars included (measured on the coach's screenshots: the bars' logo and icons at 72–96pt read blurred, everything from ~100pt sharp); there, and only there (the page is under the clock: `--ios-edge-room` = `min(28px, env(safe-area-inset-top) × 100)`, 0 under a solid status bar), the room is added into `--app-safe-top` and `--focus-top`, and deleting the app and adding it again from Safari gives it the solid bar: every top bar, its spacer, the full-height chats (`--app-top-chrome`) and the toasts start below the blur, and the band only holds the background (`--app-edge-room` is that band where it's in effect: 0 in View as client and everywhere else; Crew's paged feed lets a post run up through it, the paged-feed rule); the View as client rule still wins (the banner holds the notch). On those same old installs iOS ends its fixed layout where the page ends and draws nothing below it (only the page's background), while the window measures a status bar short (793pt of an 852pt screen): so the shell is at least the whole screen tall there (`[data-shell-root]` `min-height: min(100vh, var(--app-vh) + env(safe-area-inset-top) × 1000)`: the measured window only under a solid status bar, 100vh under a transparent one), never just the window, or every page that doesn't scroll (Messages) ends at 793pt and takes the tab bar 59pt up with it. Nothing is ever moved below that edge (moving the tab bar down into it cut its labels off). As a safety net `watchIosBottomGap` (ios-edge.ts, started by AppShell) reads any gap that's left from a persistent fixed probe (`iosBottomGap`: the screen's height less where a fixed box ends, only in the home-screen app under a transparent status bar, on a phone, upright, never more than about the status bar's height, `IOS_GAP_SLACK`; a ResizeObserver, coming back and settle timers follow iOS flipping layouts), and `--ios-bottom-gap` / `data-ios-bottom-gap` only tuck the tab bar: it gives up as much of the home indicator's room as the band below already covers (`--mobile-bar-pad`, the bar's padding, and `--mobile-bar-top` with it), so it stays whole and as low as it can, never while typing. Its light / dark switch is neutral (`bg-secondary`, no tint). The native build turns UIKit's scroll edge effect off on the web view (`topEdgeEffect` / `bottomEdgeEffect.isHidden`, patched into AppDelegate by ios-build.yml, Xcode 26 / iOS 26+ only). Full-height fixed screens (the coach inbox and thread, the client chat) start at `--app-top-chrome` (the banner + the notch + the bar's row), never `--shell-topbar-h` alone, and on md+ the sidebar and the page are padded by `env(safe-area-inset-top)`; page-level `sticky` bars keep `top-0`, because `<main>` (`overflow-x-hidden`) is their scroll box and an offset there only pushes them down — why: on the coach's iPhone the Messages tabs and the Crew bar showed blurred under the status bar, the chats covered the app's top bar on notched iPhones, and on an iPad the sidebar and page titles sat under the clock; and on Nov 14 2026 the coach found the tab bar floating 60pt above the bottom of the screen with a dead band under it ("the alignment is off"), and then its icons sliced off and its labels gone on Messages ("the navigation at the bottom glitches"), once the bar had been moved into a band iOS doesn't draw; and on Nov 13 2026 the coach found the empty band between the clock and every top bar wasted space (TikTok's header sits right under the clock), which only a solid status bar can give a home-screen web app, since iOS 26.1's blur over a transparent one can't be turned off from the page.
- Workouts and Nutrition open from the phone's copy: a client's program is one query for every screen (`clientWorkoutsQuery`, client-workouts-query.ts, key `["my-workouts", clientId]`: Workouts, Home's calendar via calendar-sources, Nutrition's training-day check, the cardio card and the grocery list; `primaryWorkouts` where at-home backup sessions never counted), and it and the pages' first reads (portal-page-queries.ts: `portalWorkoutsClientQuery`, `portalNutritionCtxQuery`, `coachMealPlanQuery`) keep `gcTime: PAGE_CACHE_MS` (= `QUERY_PERSIST_MAX_AGE`), so the copy saved on the phone survives to the next open and the page paints at once, refreshing behind; the app's 10-minute default dropped them before they were saved. `useWarmClientPages` (portal layout) fetches them ~1.5s after the app paints, once idle, never while previewing as a client, the coach's plan for coaching clients only, and only what's older than `WARM_FRESH_MS`. While something loads, the page keeps its title and shows a placeholder in the shape it opens into (`ScheduleSkeleton` / `WorkoutCardSkeleton` / `ProgramCardSkeleton` / `WorkoutsBodySkeleton`, `MealPlanCardSkeleton` / `TargetsCardSkeleton`: the meal plan's spot is held so it never drops in above the targets), never a spinner or "Loading…" line; `Skeleton` is neutral `bg-muted` with one soft sweep (`jf-skeleton`, still under reduced motion), and content that replaced a placeholder fades in once (`useArrivalClass` → `jf-arrive`, opacity only: a transform would trap `position: fixed` children) — why: opening Nutrition downloaded the whole program twice (several hundred KB each for a client with a few blocks of history), Home's calendar a third time under its own key, nothing survived to the next app open, and both pages loaded in stages behind a spinner and a red-tinted pulse while the meal plan pushed the page down when it arrived.
- Workout posts in Crew (a finished workout with no photo, `WorkoutHero`; a Pulse, `PulseHero`; a lock in, `LockInHero`; their grid tiles and Home cards; a post shown in a chat, Saved or Repost; the after-workout preview) are a plain surface that follows the theme (`bg-muted`, `text-foreground`, `text-muted-foreground`), never a glow or gradient: green (`text-success`) says done, amber (`text-warning`) a PR, and a win's colour is `WIN_STYLE[kind].tone` (`.text` only on the always-dark story frame, `.tint` is always empty). On a post's painted card a lesson's diagram is drawn in its paper colours (`LESSON_PAPER_COLORS`, `tone="paper"`; stories keep `dark`), and a chart mark that isn't the highlight is a solid grey (`ink(0.42)`+), never a faint wash. Everything that draws a post as a picture matches: the studio's workout cards and their share / Instagram files (`paintShareCard`) sit on one flat charcoal with no photo (`plainBackdrop`, `CARD_BACKDROP`; a PR is solid amber, `gold`), the workout and recap story cards, a words post added to a story (`postShareCardItem`), the lesson story frame and the repost editor are flat (the only gradients left are scrims that dim a photo under text), a card shows nothing (never blank paper) until it's ready, and lesson illustrations are drawn on the same paper — why: the coach found the red / gold glows on clients' workout posts neither clean nor aesthetic, and the cards' diagrams were still drawn in the story's white, so they disappeared on the light paper; the previews (the studio, Share to Instagram, Add to story, a story) still painted the old red / gold glow after the feed had dropped it.
- A page nobody uses is removed, not hidden: decided from the data (rows, last write, who wrote them), its code, menu / tab / palette / search entries and any popup or Home read it fed are deleted, and its route file stays only as a `beforeLoad` redirect to the closest live page (never a 404; old pushes, bookmarks and `/m/*` links land somewhere real); the database tables stay as history. Removed Nov 2026: Announcements / Broadcasts (client page, Communication tab, composer, the popup on every portal page; 0 ever sent), Resources (client + member pages and the Tools list; one "Configure Fillout" placeholder ever), Coaching → Requests and Home's request popup (0 ever), Calendar → Events and the event popup (three blank "New Event"s; a single event still opens at `/admin/events/$id`), Team → Operations (links to placeholders), the "coming soon" pages (SOPs, Stripe Sync, Content Ideas, Testimonials, Programs, Challenges, Welcome Messages, Promo Tools), Launch Readiness (membership is live), Refund Policy (a second door to Checkout settings) and Legacy Migration (Trainerize move done). Removed Nov 5 2026: the client's Check-Ins & Forms list (forms are sent and answered in the coach chat; its Check-In Responses last written Aug 13; `/portal/check-ins` → Messages, while a form still opens at `/portal/check-ins/$formId`, `check-ins_.$formId.tsx`, never nested under a page without an outlet), the client's Agreements page (Account's Coaching Agreement card again; `/portal/agreements` lands there and `?sign=1` still opens signing), native agreement packages and guest signing (`/admin/agreements-native*`, `/portal/agreements-native/*`, `/sign/$token`; 0 ever sent or signed) and Training Phases (7 rows, all Jun 9; Home and the nav badges no longer read `training_phases` or `media_comments`); set-once pages (CRM, membership setup pages, Popups, Onboarding, Feature Flags, Archive Manager) are `searchOnly`. `unused-pages-removed.test.ts` holds the list — why: the coach found screens like Announcements that were never used cluttering the menus, and two popups queried empty tables on every client app open.
- Every chat list (the coach inbox, the client's Messages, Requests, 1:1s, member groups, coach-run groups) draws its rows with `ChatListRow` (chat-list-row.tsx), at Instagram's size: a 56px face (`CHAT_ROW_AVATAR`), the name at 16px (regular when read, semibold when unread), the latest message at 15px with its age after it ("· 5m", `chatAge`), ~72px rows, no dividers, and Instagram's blue dot for unread (`UNREAD_DOT`, never the brand red); the coach's signals ride as its third line — why: the lists were 36–44px faces with 12–14px text and read too small next to Instagram, and every list had its own copy of the row.
- Every coaching application is a lead with the AI's read and a text to send (coaching-leads.ts, 20270121090000_coaching_lead_replies.sql): the database queues it on insert (`coaching_lead_replies_queue`, never a test) and the `lead-replies-tick` hook writes it in the background (`runQueuedLead`: one conditional-update claim, a newer Write again wins, 3 tries; pg_cron every 5 minutes while one waits), never inside the applicant's or the coach's request; the first one pushes the coach (`lead_ready`, category `coaching_apps`, opens `/admin/leads?id=`), and the SMS / email alert links there too. The model sees only their own answers and the live discovery-call link (`leadBookingUrl`; never their score) and writes, in the coach's voice (`voicePromptForClient`, `casualize`), a Cardone-style first text (certainty, one next step, an either/or time, never a yes/no) and a follow-up; any link but the booking one is stripped (`keepOnlyBookingLink`). `leadNeeds` is the one reading of what's waiting (a first text within `LEAD_WAITING_DAYS`, or a follow-up a day after "I sent it" until a call is booked) for the Leads page, Home's `NewLeadsCard` (hidden at zero) and the `LeadsInboxRow` pinned under Cleo in Messages (admin only); unfinished applications get `draftNudge`, no AI. `coaching_lead_replies` is admin / admin-viewer read only and written only by the server — why: Devin applied on Nov 3 and the only signal was an SMS and an email pointing at a list, with nothing in the app and no help writing the text that gets a lead on a call while they're hot.
- A link to any page, sent anywhere (iMessage, texts, Instagram, WhatsApp, Messenger), shows a short, clean card: `shareMeta` (share-meta.ts) writes every page's `og:` / `twitter:` title and line ("JF Effect", "Book a time with JF Effect", "JF Effect Coaching"; ≤ `SHARE_TITLE_MAX`, never a place name, keyword or "|"), and the root adds the logo once (`shareImageMeta`: icon-1024.png with its size); no page types its own share tags, and `og:url` is only ever the page's own address. Keyword titles for Google stay in each page's `<title>` / description — why: every page without its own card (the booking link texted to leads) showed "JF Effect | Personal Trainer Selkirk MB & Online Fitness Coaching", and the root's `og:url` pointed every share at the home page.
- JF Effect is free to join (20270128090000_free_platform.sql): `/join` (web and in the app; `/membership` and `/signup/jf` redirect there) is `FreeSignup` (first name, email, password; `checkFreeSignup`, free-signup.ts), a Supabase sign-up with `jf_signup: "free"` whose confirmation email lands on `/welcome` (scanner-safe, `SAFE_PATHS`; the token is spent only on Continue). Confirming makes the free member: the `auth_user_free_join` trigger and `/welcome` (and `useServiceTier`'s self-heal) call `member_free_join` (idempotent: a confirmed login only, never staff, nobody who already has a client row) → an `app_members` row (`jf_member`, `subscription_status = 'Free'`), default access and the self-serve client row. `handle_new_user` links a coach's client record by email only for an invited (`invited_at`) or staff-made (confirmed at creation) login, and `member_free_join` answers `coach_account` instead of making a second record when a coach already set that email up. A free member's Membership page is the free state with Apply for coaching; the paid checkout code stays but nothing links to it. A free account's setup (`MemberSetupGate` / `MemberSetupWizard`) is two steps, a goal + experience and the days they train (`setupStepsFor` / `setupMissing`, member-setup-steps.ts, and the database's `member_setup_complete`, 20270129120000_free_member_setup.sql, the same rule): never the paid membership's ID photo, phone, date of birth, address or emergency contact, and Account's "N things to finish" asks a member only for the unit and training days (`profileMissing(…, coachingIntake)`: a coaching client's intake, the rest, is the coach's; a member's photo and personal details stay there to add, never flagged); its last step also asks pounds or kilograms (`applyWeightUnitChoice`, so "Pounds or kilograms?" never follows it), and the launch popups (Add to Home Screen, the unit prompt) wait while setup is loading or unfinished and until the account's tier is known (`setupStillOpen(state, tier)`: a brand-new member's setup gate mounts only once the app knows they're self-serve, and before #817 the unit sheet opened in that gap with setup on top of it). Someone whose coaching application (a `Lead` client row with no coach) matches the email they join with keeps that one record: `_member_free_join` links it before making the member row and `ensure_member_client` turns it self-serve (20270201090000_free_platform_hardening.sql; the join runs as the server, so the clients guards let it through even from the person's own /welcome call). A record a coach made (anything else unlinked with that email) answers `coach_account` and makes nothing; the coach's Send invite / Copy setup link then links the login they made themselves after giving it a random password (`claimUnlinkedLogin`, clients.functions.ts), so only whoever opens the email gets in. A sign-up's confirmation link lands on /welcome even if Supabase dropped its redirect (`scannerSafeAuthLink`). Supabase Auth's "Confirm email" must stay on: `handle_new_user` links by email any login confirmed at creation, which an auto-confirmed public sign-up would be — why: nobody had ever kept a paid membership (one member, cancelled in June), so the paywall only kept people out of the crew; and `handle_new_user` linked any public sign-up to a coaching client's record by email before the email was confirmed, so whoever registered a client's email first got their coaching data the moment the real client tapped "Confirm"; and the paid setup asked every new free member for a face photo "for ID verification" and their home address before the app would stop nagging.
- JF Effect is free, by application (Nov 19 2026, 20270420090000_membership_applications.sql, membership-apply.ts pure and tested): jfeffect.com's "/" is the landing page for signed-out visitors (`LandingPage`, landing-page.tsx: real app screens from scripts/coaching-screenshots' made-up athlete (landing.cjs re-shoots the landing page's five), never a client; true numbers only) and the app's launch for anyone signed in or in the installed app, decided before the first paint by `LAUNCH_BOOT_SCRIPT` (landing-launch.ts, `html[data-launch="app"]` in styles.css; `?landing` forces the page), so the app never flashes a marketing page. /join is the application (`MembershipApplication`: goal, experience, coaching interest (optional), name, how to reach them (Instagram DM, text, email), then only the details that way needs, email always; no account or password; answers kept on the phone; honeypot, a 2.5 s floor and 5 an hour per hashed address, a bot gets the same answer and nothing is saved) into `membership_applications` (server only: no client policies; one pending per email, applying again updates it). Nobody becomes a member until approved: `_member_free_join` asks `_membership_join_allowed` before linking or making anything (logins made before `app_settings.membership_applications_since` keep the old rule), and a login with no athlete record sees `MembershipPendingGate` (useServiceTier `noAccount`), never empty screens. The coach works them in Applications (/admin/leads, Members · Coaching; the Messages row and Home card show while anyone is waiting; one push per burst, `rateKey` member_application): answers, the Instagram profile a tap away, DM / Text / Email with his "say hi" words (`hiMessage`, no AI), contact status, private notes, Flag, Decline (sends nothing) and Approve, which emails `membership-approved` with the activation link and hands back the same link to DM or text (`approvedMessage`). /activate (`ActivatePage`): the emailed link carries the email proof (`emailProof`) and goes straight to a password; a DM'd or texted link emails a 6-digit code first (`sendActivationCode`, hashed, 15 min, 5 tries), so a link that reaches the wrong person can't take an inbox; the login is made unconfirmed then confirmed, so `handle_new_user` never hands it a coach's record, and the join stamps the application and fills setup's goal and experience from it; then Jared's welcome (`welcomeNote`) and one first move (`firstMove`: a program for their goal and level). The funnel (anonymous `membership_funnel_events`, a random id per browser, once a day per step; `membership_funnel`) is the Members tab's How people join card: visited, started, sent, approved, joined, trained in week one, drop-off by question and sources — why: on Nov 19 2026 the coach wanted the most people to want to try JF Effect and apply, a personal hello before access (text first, a video call only if they want one), and to see the funnel; free sign-up let anyone straight in, and "/" sent visitors to a sign-in screen.
- The public site is a coaching brand with its own app, and says so in Jared's words (Nov 20 2026, landing-page.tsx, coaching.index.tsx, about.tsx, landing-free-vs-coaching.test.ts). The home page is told like a product launch (Nov 23 2026): the promise and the product ("Train like you have a coach.", the hero's own two screens, Home and a workout in Focus mode, `HeroDevices`; Get the free app the red `PrimaryCta`, Explore coaching outlined beside it, scrolling to `#coaching`), the proof strip (only numbers checked against the records: medals and titles as the About page says them, people coached, the 15 live programs), the app one capability at a time, each on its own current screen (`CHAPTERS`: Programs, Logging, Progress, Nutrition; then your own program, `data-landing-own`: Build your own and Upload one you already have; bar speed in the free list, typed from a tracker, and the bar path and speed read from a set video only under coaching; set videos are coaching's and never listed as free), then the reveal (`FreeReveal`, `data-landing-reveal`: what was shown said in one line, then the app's price as its own moment, "$0" from the same `priceLabel` as every price, the largest type in that panel settling in like the coaching price does, then "The JF Effect app is free." and Get the free app; the anchor is that it's the same app the coaching clients train in, true; never a "worth" figure, a crossed-out price or a fake discount; seeing it is the anonymous `free_reveal_view` step, "Saw the free app's $0"), then "The app gives you the tools. Coaching gives you a coach." and online coaching as its own premium section, never a "Paid" column in a price table (`data-landing-coaching`: "I'll run your training.", what Jared does in five rows: program, weekly check-ins, nutrition, technique and questions with the coach chat's own "I usually reply within a day", meet prep; the check-in chat and a coach-written meal plan as the pictures; See the packages while any are listed), then the coaching price revealed the way the iPad's was (Nov 23 2026, `PriceRevealSection`, price-reveal.tsx; `priceReveal`, price-reveal.ts, pure and tested in price-reveal.test.ts): first what a personal trainer costs, as a worked example from a published public rate (`trainerAnchor`, trainer-cost.ts: the City of Mississauga's 2026 rate at its 8-or-more tier, $56.23 an hour before HST, below every single-session public rate checked; twice a week is $487 a month, $5,848 a year, before tax, said beside "What does a personal trainer cost?" with the source in one line; "Where these numbers come from" is a native `<details>` with the maths, once and three times a week, every public rate checked with its link, lower bundles included, the day it was checked and that private gyms set their own prices; shown only while a month of it costs more than the higher coaching price after it; never a premium gym's price, an estimate, a competitor named for its price or a "you save"), then the honest difference ("A trainer is with you for the hours you book. I coach your whole week.", two cards: a trainer is beside you for every rep of the sessions you book and what happens between them depends on the trainer; online with me, I'm not in the room, I write every session, read your check-ins and review the sets you film; the FAQ's "Is it the same as a personal trainer?" says No and why), then Coaching Plus, at its own price ("Everything I do as your coach, plus time with me on video.", its calls as two "30 min call" chips and `callsLine`, then $249/month large), then "Don't need the private calls? Get the same coaching without them.", then Complete Online Coaching's price as the largest thing on the page in a dark panel ("Everything in Coaching Plus except the two calls.", Apply for coaching with `from=reveal`, Want the calls? Apply for Coaching Plus, Compare every option). Real prices for different services, never a discount: the trainer example is labelled as one and never called the same thing as coaching, and the two coaching packages are no crossed-out or "former" price, no "save" / "only" / "off", no number ticking from one price to the other, and the $249 never shown beside the $130; it shows only while `priceReveal` says every word is true (the recommended monthly package has no calls of its own, a listed package above it says "Everything in" it and adds nothing but calls, the nearest such one is the anchor), else the page goes straight on. Motion is a reveal, never a gate (`useStage`, `motion`, `PRICE_SETTLE`, stage-motion.ts, shared by the $0 and every stage of the story): everything is server-rendered and readable with no script, a stage still below the screen once the page is interactive eases in the first time it's scrolled to (the $130 last, a settle from slightly smaller and soft focus), a stage already on screen (a deep link, a reload half way down) is never held back, reduced motion has no motion, nothing locks scrolling or waits on a timer, and the hero's See plans and prices still jumps straight to the cards. Seeing it is one anonymous step (`price_reveal_view`, the admin funnel's "Saw the price reveal", 20270515100000_price_reveal_funnel.sql) so applications before and after it can be compared; one client's words where they make the point, the coaching packages with their prices (the next rule; the recommended card carries one honest anchor, `anchorLine`: how much more it is than the next package on the site, both listed prices, e.g. "$31 more than Nutrition Coaching", and nothing when there's nothing cheaper listed; one-time calls are never on the home page, `calls={false}`; Nov 23 2026: the section is every way in side by side, the free app and every listed monthly package by price (`optionCards`, packages.ts, with `free`: never a draft or an unlisted one, so the database decides what shows), four across on a computer, two by two on a tablet, stacked on a phone, the recommended one inverted and raised (`xl:-my-4`), `compact` cards when there are four; it's the page's `#pricing`, reached from the hero's "See plans and prices" (`PLANS_JUMP`, `data-landing-jump`, in the first screen on every size) and the header's Pricing, its note saying applying never charges; /coaching keeps the paid packages and the deeper explanation, never a stop needed to compare), client photos and words, an FAQ, and the decision ("Which one is you?", `decisionRows`: the free app then each listed monthly package by price, each row the package's own "Best for" in the visitor's words, so a package taken off the site leaves it). Every app screen on the public pages is the app itself, captured by scripts/coaching-screenshots (landing.cjs: app-home, app-focus, app-week, app-log, app-insights, app-food, app-build, app-import; run.cjs: app-program, app-overview, app-coach-chat, app-nutrition; a made-up athlete, never a client), re-captured whenever the app's look changes, and no screen is shown twice on one page. Coaching is marketed online only on every public page: no town, gym or in-person training in any page, title, description or schema (the trainer example names the public providers its rates come from as sources, never as where JF Effect trains) (the root's schema is an `Organization`, never a `LocalBusiness`); /personal-trainer-selkirk, its /apply and /selkirk redirect to /coaching and /coaching/apply (keeping `?from=`), the in-person services themselves (booking types, PT sessions) unchanged. The words are Jared's own, written the way he'd write them: first person everywhere (the home page leads "Train like you have a coach.", About opens "Hey, I'm Jared.", the join and activation screens say "I read every application", "Message me"; never "Jared reads…" or "he'll" about himself, outside the consent line that has to name who contacts them), plain and confident, no fitness-marketing or AI lines (the test lists the ones Jared called out: "Real programs", "Structure beats motivation", "the paid part", "actually prep", "Real results", "Spots are limited", "unlock", "journey"…), never the words "real", "really" or "actually" ("remove real from your vocabulary"), no dashes on the home page, no promise of unlimited access. The navigation is one list (public-nav.ts, pure, tested in public-nav.test.ts; `PublicHeader`, public-header.tsx, for every `SalesPageShell`): the JF Effect logo is Home, then Home · Free app (`/#app`) · Online coaching · Pricing (`/#pricing`, lit while the home page's options are what's on screen) · About, Sign in quiet, and the page's one red button (`headerCtaFor`: Get the free app on the home page, Apply for coaching on /coaching and /about) on a tablet or computer; a phone gets the logo and a menu (`PhoneMenu`: the five with a line each saying which is free, which is paid and where they're compared, the page's button, Sign in), its asks being the page's own buttons and the bottom bar; which row is lit is `activeNavKey`'s (/join lights Free app; the router's own active marking is held to the exact address so Home and Free app never light together). While the home page's coaching section (`data-cta-zone="coaching"`) fills the middle of the screen the header's button and the phone's bottom bar (`StickyMobileCta zones`, sticky-cta.ts) ask for the coaching application, never the free app beside it; the bottom bar steps aside while a page's own button is on screen (`hideWhenInline`, `data-inline-cta`), the coaching page and About only ever ask for the application, and the applications themselves (/join, /coaching/apply) get a quiet header (`hideMarketingNav`: the logo and Sign in) Every public page's photos can't be saved from the page (Nov 20 2026, `SalesPageShell` `data-protect-media`, protect-media.ts, the matching rule in styles.css): no right-click or Android long-press menu and no drag on a photo or video, no iOS long-press callout, while text keeps its menu; a screenshot still works, nothing a page does stops one. About's two "leveled-up" comparisons (leveled-up-12, leveled-up-4) carry a caption bar 87% of the way down and are zoomed past it from a point 32% down (`CAPTION_BAR_CROP`), so their top stays where it was. — why: on Nov 20 2026 the coach found the site's copy still sounded AI-written ("Structure beats motivation", "A crew that notices your PRs", "The app is free. Coaching with me is the paid part"), wanted coaching marketed online only and sold as a premium service rather than a feature checklist under a "Paid" heading, and found the navigation unclear (JF Effect as the only way home, nothing saying which part is free, Join free beside the coaching application); and on Nov 23 2026 that the page showed value before price but never set a credible higher price for the flagship to be revealed against, the moment that made the iPad's $499 land, which the genuine $249 Plus package gives honestly; then that the price story should start from what people already know a coach costs (a personal trainer, hundreds a month and thousands a year) with transparent, defensible examples that never exaggerate or pretend online coaching is the same thing, and that the free app deserved a memorable $0 of its own.
- What JF Effect sells is one source of truth, `coaching_packages` (Nov 22 2026, 20270505090000_coaching_packages.sql, src/lib/coaching-packages/: packages.ts and call-period.ts and call-prep.ts pure and tested in coaching-packages.test.ts; the admin's Sales → Coaching packages, `CoachingSalesToolkit`): each package's name, one-liner, what's included, advertised price (CAD before tax), status (draft · active · archived), whether it's listed on the site (`is_public`, only while active, a paid one only with a price: checked in the database), its order and one recommended package. The landing page's `#pricing` and /coaching's are drawn from the listed ones by one component (`CoachingPackagesGrid` / `PackageCard`, the same card the editor previews, so the preview is what goes live), every price with "CAD, plus applicable tax" (`TAX_NOTE`; the public can't ask whether tax applies), the recommended one in the inverted card with "Recommended" (never "Most Popular"), each button to the application with the package picked (`ctaHref`: `/coaching/apply?package=<slug>`); the FAQ's cost answer is `costAnswer` (the listed packages by name and price, never a blanket "starts at"); no price is ever typed into a page (landing-free-vs-coaching.test.ts and coaching-packages.test.ts check). The public reads only `coaching_packages_public()` / `coaching_package_public(slug)` (anon-granted, public fields only, through the publishable key: never the admin note or the product; an active unlisted package is a custom offer reached by its link; a draft never), and the landing page never waits on them (the `/` loader hands a promise to `Await`; /coaching waits at most 2 s and adds them to its schema as Offers). A package's price is the advertised price: it never changes a sale, a Stripe product or a subscription (`product_id` only names the Stripe-backed product it's sold with; Complete Online Coaching is linked to the existing "Online Coaching — $130/month"); every change a visitor or client could see is kept by its trigger in `coaching_package_versions` (the pricing history), and saving a listed package asks first ("Update jfeffect.com now?"), as does putting one on or taking one off the site. Seeded Nov 22 2026: the free app and Complete Online Coaching ($130/month, recommended) listed; Nutrition Coaching ($99), Coaching Plus ($249, 2 × 30-min calls a month) and four one-time calls (no price yet) drafts, listed only when the coach approves them; the older "Coaching Plus" products in Products are unrelated legacy offers. Which package a client is on is `coaching_package_enrollments` (staff only, set by the coach from the client's Summary or a package's Clients: what they pay when it isn't the advertised price, the sale whose billing date anchors their included calls, their own number of calls; one active at a time; it mirrors the package's name into `clients.coaching_package`; nothing moves a client onto a package by itself). Included calls (a package with `included_calls`) are a video booking type with no PT credit (`call_booking_card_id`, "Coaching Plus Call", active only while the package is or someone's on it, `coaching_call_card_sync`), listed and bookable only for a client on that package (`includedCallTypesFor`, `includedCallCheck` in `createOnlineBooking`) and enforced by the database under a per-client lock (`pt_sessions_included_call_guard`, a client's own booking only, never the coach's): a billing period runs a month from the anchor's day, clamped (`callPeriod` = `coaching_call_period`), never the calendar month; Scheduled, Completed and Missed calls count, a cancelled one goes back, unused ones don't carry over. The client sees them on Home (`IncludedCallsCard`: calls left until the period ends, Book a call, what they want to cover, `coaching_call_topics`, server-written, and the next steps the coach sent); the coach opens "Call prep and next steps" on any call's session sheet (`CallPrepSheet`, `getCallPrep` with the coach's own login): their words, last call's follow-ups, what's on file (each line from a record) and, kept apart and labelled, the app's read (interpretations, never a diagnosis), then action items and next priorities (`coaching_call_reviews`, staff only) that reach the client only when the coach taps Send (a chat message; the private note is the session's own note and never sent). Share cards are painted from the packages on a canvas (`paintPricingCard`, pricing-card.ts: one package or a comparison, post 4:5 · square · story; the preview is the exported image, PNG through `saveFiles` and a one-page PDF), a draft stamped DRAFT and never shareable; Copy text (`pricingText`) and Copy application link (`shareLink`). An application keeps the package it was for (`coaching_applications.package_slug` / `package_snapshot`, its name in `coaching_interest`, shown on the Leads card); applying is never a sale or a price promise and says so — why: on Nov 22 2026 the coach asked for one premium coaching offer at $130 a month with the proposed $99 and $249 tiers ready but not public until he approves them, a pricing section and application path built on it, shareable pricing cards, Coaching Plus calls booked against a real per-billing-period allowance with a prep overview and post-call next steps, and nothing changing an existing client's price or subscription.
- The coaching lineup and how the site is measured (Nov 23 2026, 20270513090000_coaching_site_launch.sql, site-funnel-rules.ts pure and tested in coaching-site.test.ts): four offers, the free app, Nutrition Coaching ($99, nutrition only: no programming, technique feedback or competition coaching), Complete Online Coaching ($130, the flagship, recommended) and Coaching Plus ($249: everything in Complete plus exactly two private 30-minute video calls each billing month, nothing else), all CAD plus applicable tax; Nutrition and Plus went on the site on Nov 23 2026 once each could be sold (the next sentence), and the four one-time consultations (Technique Review $79 / 30 min, Training Consultation $119 / 45, Nutrition Review $99 / 45, Competition Strategy $179 / 60) are priced drafts that stay off the site until their booking and payment are set up and tested. A monthly package is made sellable by the `coaching-package-stripe` hook (stripe-setup.ts pure and tested, stripe-setup.server.ts; cron secret; a preview unless `apply`): its Stripe price found by lookup key (`packageLookupKey`, `jf_coaching_pkg_<slug>_month_<cents>_cad`) or made new (a product named for the package, one monthly CAD price before tax, idempotency keys), never an edit of an existing price or product; its Products row shaped like Complete's $130/month (`productRowFor`); the package pointing at it; then proved by a subscription checkout with Stripe Tax made and expired at once (no customer, nothing payable). 20270514090000_four_options_live.sql lists Nutrition and Plus only where that link exists and matches the package's price and currency, and moves Complete's first line to "Everything in Nutrition Coaching" only once Nutrition is listed and only from its words as they were. The four active $130 subscribers are on Complete by an enrollment pointing at their own sale (association only: their sale, Stripe subscription, billing date, price and terms are never touched). Included calls say their rule wherever they're used (`callsPolicy` on /coaching, the Home card, `IncludedCallCancelNote` when a client asks to cancel one): 24+ hours ahead gives the call back, later or a no-show uses it, a call the coach moves or cancels never counts, nothing carries over. The coach answering a client's can't-make-it on an included call sees which side of the rule it was asked on (`cancelRuling`, call-period.ts, read from when the request was sent, never when he answers; `useIncludedCallRuling` on the chat's `SessionChangeCard` and in `SessionActionsSheet`: Give it back or Count it, the one the rule points to as the main button), and still decides; the database's allowance (two a billing month, a timely cancel restoring one, a late cancel or no-show counted, a reschedule still one call, the coach's own booking over it, a coach cancel not counted, a fresh two next period, none off the package) was checked on production in a rolled-back transaction on Nov 23 2026. The public site's steps go into `membership_funnel_events` anonymously (`trackSite`, site-funnel.ts: a random id per browser, the first source, a coarse device class, once per step per day; the landing view, the coaching page view, the pricing section seen, which button and which package was tapped, every application question reached, the application sent with its package), and the admin reads them in Sales → Coaching packages → How people find it (`coaching_funnel()`, service role after an admin check), interest (taps, applications by package) kept apart from customers (an application followed by a sale or a package), by device — why: on Nov 23 2026 the coach asked for the landing page, coaching page, packages and journey to be launched like a premium product (value before price, honest anchors, the app on screen throughout, current screenshots) with the $130 package the natural choice, measured without tracking anyone, and with nothing unapproved made public.
- Programs people build (`athlete_programs`, `/portal/programs`: My programs · JF Effect · Crew, `/portal/programs/new` (+ `?edit=`), `/portal/programs/$programId`) are a plan of blocks, weeks and training days with every week written out (`athlete_programs.plan`, 20270418090000_member_programs_blocks_weeks.sql, program-plan.ts: up to 8 blocks and 24 weeks, a new one 12; library exercises only, `athlete_program_clean_plan`: a real, unarchived `exercises` id, sets 1–20, reps, RPE or RIR, a load in lb / kg or a % of their max or of today's top set, rest ≤ 15 min, tempo, a note, ≤ 7 days, ≤ 20 exercises a day; `days` / `weeks` stay its first week and length for older apps, which are refused when they'd save over a plan; a program from before plans reads as its one week repeated, `planFromDays`), written only through the `athlete_program_*` RPCs (the table shows a person only their own rows; staff all). They live apart from the coach's `pl_templates` (admins read every template, so members' would flood the coach's library, pickers and Cleo). Sharing is a switch (`visibility = 'community'`), seen by whoever `athlete_program_visible` allows (the owner; staff; else not taken down, not blocked either way, and a private account's only by its followers); Save a copy (`athlete_program_copy`) makes the saver's own private copy with `forked_from` (the original never changes; `save_count` counts each person once) and Start (`startMyProgram` → `athlete_program_start`, the owner's own copy, self-serve with access only) writes a `pl_block` per block the member owns (`created_by`) on the same engine (more than one under a `pl_preps`, the first Active and the rest Draft, like a library plan; `athlete_program_insert_rows` writes RPE, RIR, tempo, the % and its basis and the load), archives the program they started before (in `athlete_program_start` itself, so a direct call never stacks live programs; at most 10 starts a day) and places the workouts on their training days like a library plan (`StartPlanDialog startWith`). Starts count once per person (`athlete_program_starts`), on the program and the original it was copied from. Browsing (`athlete_programs_browse`) reads the viewer, their blocks and follows once as sets, the same rules as `athlete_program_visible` (internal: not executable by `authenticated`); "Based on a program by @X" is left off across a block. Exercises live in `days` as library ids: a merged duplicate moves with it (`exercise_merge_athlete_programs` on `exercise_dedupe_audit`), the exercise delete guard counts programs, and a save names an exercise that left the library. A coaching client can build, copy and share, never start: their coach writes the program — why: the builder and the crew's programs are why people use JF Effect without a coach, copies must never change someone else's work, and members' programs must never mix into the coach's templates.
- A program someone already has comes in through the importer (Nov 21 2026, src/lib/program-import/, src/components/program-import/, `/portal/programs/import`, 20270427090000_program_imports.sql; program-import.test.ts) and ends as the builder's own plan (`athlete_programs`, saved through the same `athlete_program_save_plan`), private until they share it. A spreadsheet (.xlsx: cells as shown, % formats as percentages, merged cells, hidden sheets skipped; .xls asks for .xlsx), CSV, Word (.docx: every heading above a table kept) or pasted text is read on the phone (extract.ts, fflate) and parsed by code first (parse.ts: weeks, week ranges repeated and said, blocks, days, supersets, sets × reps, RPE / RIR, %, loads, rest, tempo, velocity, progression in words kept as words, a bare exercise name brought in for its numbers, a number in a column it can't place flagged, never dropped); only what the parser can't read whole (`confident`), a PDF or photos (uploaded to the private `program-imports` bucket, photos as JPEG) or a re-read the person asks for goes to AI, in the background on the OpenAI key (route `program_import`, staged background calls through the `program-imports-tick` hook, woken by the queue trigger and pg_cron), 10 AI credits (`ai_credit_cost` 'program_import', given back when it fails or is cancelled, 12 imports a day). The AI only copies (`IMPORT_SYSTEM`): never a value the file doesn't have, its answer checked by code (`readAiImport`) and against the file's text (`groundingFlags`). The review is the builder itself (`ProgramWorkbench review`): exercises matched to the library with the pickers' search (`matchExerciseNames`; a close match marked, nothing close left for the person to pick, one pick fixing every place the file used that name, `resolveName`; never a new exercise), a missing number left missing (planIssue names the exercise), each note said once with how many places, one-week programs offered as 4 / 8 / 12 weeks, the file's text a tap away, and nothing saved until Save. Before anything is read the person ticks `IMPORT_RIGHTS_TEXT`, stored with the import (`rights_text`, `rights_confirmed_at`); sharing an imported program asks again (`IMPORTED_SHARE_WARNING`). Files are deleted on save or discard and within 7 days; an unsaved import after 30, a saved one keeping only its record (`program_imports`: own rows read only, server-written) — why: on Nov 21 2026 the coach asked for a fast, accurate importer for programs people already follow (PDF, Excel, CSV, Word, text, photos) that preserves the structure, never invents, flags what's unclear, and keeps uploads private and only programs people have the rights to.
- Every way to a program starts in one place (Nov 21 2026, `ProgramStartOptions`, program-start-options.tsx): Follow a program (a JF member's JF Effect plans) · Build my own · Import a program, on Workouts' empty state, Home's "Pick your program", My programs with nothing saved and the Programs page's New program sheet, and all three end in the same plan, tracked the same way. Build my own asks the shape first (`NewProgram`: a name, 4 / 8 / 12 / 16 weeks or any 1–24, 1–7 days a week → `newPlan(weeks, days)`), skipped when a half-built one is waiting on the device — why: on Nov 21 2026 the coach asked for "Follow a Program | Build My Own | Import a Program" as the first-start choices and for a new program to start from its length and weekly frequency.
- A JF member's programs live on one page, `/portal/programs` (`ProgramsHub`), tabs in the address (`?tab=mine|jf|crew`, so Back returns to the tab), named for what's in them (My programs · JF Effect plans · Crew programs; Mine · JF Effect · Crew under 360px) with one quiet line under them saying what the tab holds, and every card says who it's from on top (a JF Effect plan's "JF Effect plan" with a check, `data-program-source="jf"`; a crew program's author photo and @handle, `data-program-source="crew"`; yours its status: On your calendar, Shared, Private, Copied from @x): My programs is what's on their calendar first (`OnYourCalendar`: the Workouts card's header with where it came from over the name and Manage beside it, then Go to your workouts), then their programs by source as sections (Built by you, Copied from the crew, Saved JF Effect plans: `member_plan_saved`, read back through `useSavedPlans`), and the three ways to get one when there's nothing; JF Effect is the coach-written library (`PlanLibraryBody`, self-serve only; `/portal/plans` redirects to it, a plan's page stays `/portal/plans/$planId`); Crew is what's shared. Every program says where it came from in the same words (`originLabel`, my-program.ts: "JF Effect plan", "Built by you", "Your copy of @x's program", "Written by your coach"), read from the blocks (`programOwnership`: `pl_blocks.source_template_id` for a plan, `source_athlete_program_id` for their own, written by `athlete_program_start`). The program on their calendar is theirs to manage only when they own every block of it (`created_by`): Manage beside the program's name, on the Workouts card and the hub alike (a labelled pill, never a hidden ⋯; a finished one's card ends in "Choose your next program" instead), opens `ManageProgramSheet` (a bottom sheet with a grip that swipes down and Done: the name with where it came from and its progress, then Edit program for one they built, See the plan for a JF Effect plan, Switch program and Pause program as one group, and End program apart under them with where it goes said under it, `data-manage-end`; Training days is Workouts' ⋯ only, one door); End and Pause are `member_program_end` (End asked first without `_confirm` so the sheet says how many upcoming workouts come off and how many logged ones stay, `endProgramCopy`; Pause one tap; both with Undo on the toast; the whole program, its `pl_preps` siblings too, archived into the program history (the next rule), never deleted; owner only), and editing a program they're running updates the calendar (`syncRunningProgram` → `athlete_program_sync_running`: only workouts not past, not opened and with nothing logged, `pl_day_touched`, change; days and weeks added or removed; the cardio plan's row stays; then realign and cardio sync, and a days-a-week change offers `/portal/workouts?days=1`). Starting any program is one sheet (`StartPlanDialog`, start-plan-dialog.tsx: a JF Effect plan, a program they built, a crew copy) that says before the tap what happens (`switchCopy`, my-program.ts: "Switch to X", the program that moves to the program history with its upcoming workouts that come off the calendar and its finished ones that stay, read from `member_program_end` without `_confirm`; "Start over" for the same one; a coach's program is never said to end), picks the days and the start, and lands on Workouts with `replace: true`, a switch with Undo on the toast; a program's or plan's own page names what its button replaces (`replacesLine`, "Switch to this program"). Programs, plans, exercises and Move workouts light the Workouts tab (`WORKOUTS_AREA`, admin-nav.ts) and every page under it has Back (the Programs page and Move workouts too, `backTo="/portal/workouts"`), the builder's save replaces itself in history (no Back into the editor), and a member with no program sees only the way to one: Workouts' empty state and Home's card are Find a program (My programs, which lists every way) and Build your own, with no Week / Month / Block switch, days chip or empty readiness and month cards around it (`noProgram`); an empty JF Effect library says so and offers Build your own and the crew's (`data-plan-library-empty`), never a search over nothing; a rest day names the next workout, one tap to its day (`nextWorkoutAfter`). Library cards carry no red (the plan's own page has the one main action) — why: once a plan was on a member's calendar there was no way to end or change it (members can't write `pl_blocks`), editing a program they'd built never reached the workouts already on their calendar, saved JF Effect plans were stored and never shown anywhere, and the plans, the crew's programs and their own sat on two pages that linked to each other with nothing saying which program came from where; and on Nov 14 2026 the coach asked for switching programs to be effortless (starting another plan ended the running one with only a pop-up after the tap, the Programs pages lit no tab, a member with nothing on their calendar got four empty cards and a schedule switch, and the JF Effect tab showed a search and "No plans match" over an empty library).
- A member's programs are never thrown away (Nov 20 2026, 20270424090000_member_program_history.sql, my-program.ts, program-history.tsx, member-program-lifecycle.functions.ts): ending, pausing and switching all archive the whole program (`_member_program_archive`, server only: every block and its `pl_preps` record, `archived`, nothing deleted, so its logged workouts, blocks, weeks, days and dates stay exactly as they were), and every archive or un-archive of a block a member put on their calendar themselves keeps one `member_program_history` row in step (`tg_member_program_history` on `pl_blocks.archived`: keyed by the program, its `pl_preps` id else its block's, with why (`paused` / `ended` / `switched`, from `jf.program_off_reason`; a member's own start elsewhere is a switch), the day it came off their calendar on their clock (`off_on`) and `hidden_at`; a coach's program never gets one; the member reads their own rows, nobody writes them but these functions). `member_program_history()` lists them newest first with what's done, what's left (`member_program_day_open`: never opened, nothing logged, not already missed before it came off), where it picks up, its JF Effect plan while it's live and their own program while it's saved; the screens say it in one set of words (`historyStatus`: Paused / Switched / Ended / Completed and the day, `historyProgressLine`, `historyNextLine`, `historyActions`). Program history (`/portal/programs/history`, `ProgramHistoryPage`: Workouts' ⋯ and a row in My programs) opens each one in a sheet: Resume where you left off, Start over from week 1 (its plan or program through `StartPlanDialog`), See the plan, and Remove from history set apart (asks first, Undo after; `member_program_history_hide` only hides it, its blocks and logs never change). Resume is `StartPlanDialog resume` → `resumeMyProgram` → `member_program_resume` (self-serve members with access only): the program on their calendar moves to the history as a switch, and when the old dates have gone by the program is moved (its block and every block after it, their weeks, its `pl_preps` record) so the week with the next workout opens just early enough that what's already done that week sits before the date picked and the next one falls on their first training day from it; workouts still to do get a date in their new week, done and missed ones keep theirs, then `realignClientToCommittedDays` places them, exactly where the sheet previews them (`resumePreview`, program-resume-schedule.test.ts runs the engine over it); dates still ahead are left as they were, so Undo of a pause or an end (resume today) puts it back exactly. Undo of a switch is `undoProgramStart` → `member_program_undo_start`: the program just started goes only while nothing in it is opened or logged and within 30 minutes, the one it replaced comes back and their training days go back to what they were (the start functions return `replacedKey` / `previousDays`). A paused program is offered back where the empty state would be on Workouts and in My programs (`PausedProgramCard`, `pausedProgram`: only while nothing else runs). Deleting a program they built or copied is the saved program only, never what's on the calendar (`deleteSavedProgramCopy` says so; `athlete_program_delete` keeps the running copy's link and its sharing, `athlete_program_restore` is its Undo for 30 days); a JF Effect plan is only ever unsaved, someone else's program only reported.
