Every ⋯ on Workouts and a program's page is a bottom sheet on a phone (`CardMenu` with `ariaLabel` / `vertical` / `align`), and the schedule log is "Schedule changes" beside "Program history" — why: on Nov 20 2026 the coach found the membership workout page and program management cluttered and unintuitive (corner dropdowns, a Manage sheet that repeated Training days, Switch only a link and End deleting every block with nothing logged), wanted every switch and end archived with its history, a clean Program history where members restore or restart, pause and resume, a delete that's clearly the saved program and never their logs, and switching reassuring and reversible; a member's only view of past programs was a block-by-block card at the bottom of Workouts with a sort dropdown and nothing to do with it.
- The program builder (`/portal/programs/new` + `?edit=`, `ProgramBuilder` → `ProgramWorkbench`, athlete-programs/workbench/) is one screen for phones and computers (Nov 19 2026): a header (← that asks before leaving unsaved changes, the name typed in place, the length and blocks under it opening Program, Undo / Redo (⌘Z / ⇧⌘Z, `useProgramEditor`: every edit, structural ones with an Undo toast too), Save red only once there's something to save), the day as tabs (+ Day; the day's ⋯ renames it in every week, copies it to other weeks, duplicates, moves or deletes it), the day's exercises as rows of cells typed straight in like a sheet (`Cell`, program-cells.ts: Sets · Reps · RPE (or RIR) · Load (lb, kg, % of max, % of today's top set) · Rest; only a value that reads is written, `cellPatch`, Enter goes down the column, a cell different from last week has a dot, `changedFromLastWeek`), and on a phone a bar of quick values over the keyboard (`KeyboardBar`: the field's usual answers, RPE ↔ RIR, the load's kind, Next, Done); a row's ⋯ opens one sheet (Swap keeping its numbers, Progress across weeks: every week's cells for that exercise and a start + step pattern capped at RPE 10 / RIR 0, Add back-off sets at 90% of the top set, notes, tempo, move, remove). The weeks are tabs grouped by block, Sheets' way, in a bar at the bottom (the phone's tab bar and app bar step aside while it's open, `html[data-program-builder]`; on a computer it's sticky under the day) that says which weeks an edit reaches (`Edits apply to` Week 3 only / Weeks 3–12 / all of a block / every week, `scopeWeeks` / `scopeLabel`, `defaultScope`: every week while they're all the same, else this week) and outlines those tabs; its ⋯ copies the week onto weeks picked in `WeekPickerSheet` (program-editor/week-picker-sheet.tsx, the coach editor's picker too), duplicates or deletes it, and Program (`ProgramSheet`) sets the length, the blocks (add one from the last week, rename, resize, duplicate, move, delete, split into 4-week blocks) and the details. Unsaved edits are kept on the device per login and program (`draftBackupKey`, `usableBackup`: v2 drafts, a v1 one read as its week repeated, only from the same saved version, `base` = `updated_at`, 14 days) and picked up on the next open; saving a program they're running updates its untouched workouts (`athlete_program_sync_running`, program week g to the running copy's g-th week across its blocks, weeks added to the last block), and only for a self-serve member with access (20270418091000: a client moved to coaching keeps what the coach writes on the blocks they'd started, and a member without access saves the program while their calendar waits; the app then just says Saved). The program's page reads the plan a week at a time (`PlanView`, the week tabs only when weeks differ). The picker's library is read whole, page by page (`useExerciseLibraryLite`) — why: on Nov 19 2026 the coach found the builder confusing on a phone (slow, oversized, a sheet for every routine change) and wanted it as quick and flexible as Google Sheets without looking like one: a member's program was one week repeated, so nothing could progress or change by week, there were no blocks, and a phone and a computer had two different builders.
- The program builder's everyday moves are one tap or one gesture (Nov 22 2026, program-plan.ts pure and tested in program-builder-groups.test.ts; workbench/rows.tsx, sheets.tsx, program-workbench.tsx): an exercise is dragged by its handle (`ExerciseBlock`, dnd-kit: a mouse drags at once, a finger presses and holds the handle `delay: 160`, so a swipe still scrolls; arrow keys after Space for keyboards; haptics where the phone has them; the page scrolls at the edges), never Move up / Move down, and the drop goes through `reorderExercise` (the week on screen, and each other week of the scope after the same neighbour, so a week whose order differs keeps the rest of its order). An exercise can be written as set groups (`role` warmup / top / backoff, a `label` the person types, `group` shared by entries next to each other, `exerciseUnits` / `topOf` / `setGroupName`): the exercise's ⋯ adds Back-off sets (90% of the top set, the top set marked as one), Warm-up sets (60%, before the working sets) or Another set group, each with its own sets, reps, RPE, load and rest; a group's line opens its own sheet (what it is, its name, Duplicate, Progress, Remove, and for a % of the top set Round to 5 lb · 2.5 kg or 2.5 lb · 1 kg, `round`); a % of the top set shows what it comes to when the top set has a weight (`linkedLoad`, worked out from the plan so it follows the top set), "-10" typed or the keyboard bar's −5 / −10 / −15 / −20% is 90%, and a back-off with its own weight is independent. Duplicate (the exercise's ⋯, `duplicateExercise`) copies every group right under it with its own keys, in the weeks the scope names, and lights the copy; swapping or removing an exercise takes every group with it. Which weeks an edit reaches is the header's switch, in view while typing (`ScopeBar` / `scopeOptions`: This week · Rest of block (or of program) · This block · All weeks, each with its exact weeks, a choice that reaches the same weeks as another not offered twice, `offeredScope`), the reached week tabs outlined; an edit across weeks reaches only the weeks in step with the one on screen (`inStepWeeks`, worked out once per typing session), so a week given its own value (a progression, a deload) keeps it and the builder says so after ("Weeks 2–4 kept their own RPE · Change them too"). Started or synced, each working group is its own `pl_exercise_rows` row named by `purpose_label` ("Top Set", "Back-off" or the person's name), a % of the top set reads its own exercise's top set (`basis_row_id`), `load_round` 'fine' is the logger's back-off rounding (`backoffRoundStep`), and warm-up groups lead the exercise's note ("Warm-up: 2 × 5 @ 60% of top set"), never working rows (20270509090000_program_builder_set_groups.sql); an imported top set and its back-offs become one exercise (`linkTopSets`) — why: on Nov 22 2026 the coach found the builder slow on an iPhone: reordering was Move up / Move down in a sheet, duplicating an exercise meant adding it again and retyping it, a top set with back-offs was the lift written twice, the scope lived in a menu at the bottom that the keyboard hid, and an "every week" edit flattened a progression the weeks had been given.
- The coach's program library is one list (Programming → Programs, `ProgramLibrary` in admin/program-library.tsx, read through `library-view.ts`): a search, scope chips (All · On clients · Membership app · Archived, with counts; only All and, for admins, Membership app show at 0, `visibleScopes`) and one row per program: its name, one line of blocks · weeks · days a week · style from the program itself (`programLine`), who's on it now from their own copies (`usageByTemplate`: a non-archived block from it that hasn't ended, on a client who isn't archived or deactivated), and only what's news (Membership app, "Members have an older version", "N to fix" from `ProgramStatusBadge quiet`, which is silent when a program is complete, in the builder too). A tap opens the builder; the row's ⋯ holds Assign to a client, Edit, Preview, Membership app, Name and details, Make a copy, Archive and Delete (a sheet that says clients keep their own copies). A program is in the membership app when its listing is live (`member_plans` Published + live, `membershipByTemplate`), never a tag (the `membership-app` tag is gone): one sheet, `MembershipListingSheet`, from the row's ⋯ and the builder's ⋯, sets the title, description, level, minutes and "Show it first" (`featured`), and Add / Save / Update for members each publish the program as it is now (`audience_mode` all_active, so free members see it); archiving or deleting a listed program takes it out first, and `listMembershipLibrary` never lists one whose program is gone. There is no second library: /admin/membership/programs, /admin/member-plans (and /new, /$planId), /admin/membership-library, /admin/program-submissions and /coach/programs redirect into it, and the Membership menu has no program rows (`/admin/member-plans` is only a search shortcut). Team sharing, submissions, owner / Team Live badges, tags, weight classes and template types other than a block or a multi-block program stay out of it (one admin writes every program, and none was ever shared, submitted or made as a week / day / row) — why: on Nov 13 2026 the coach found the library confusing (way too many buttons and filters, and separate ways in for coaching and for membership) and asked for a fresh canvas for both, so 20270313090000_program_library_fresh_start.sql removed the 78 templates no client was on (kept: the 10 behind a program a client is training now) and all 40 membership listings (no member had trained one), every row backed up first in `program_library_cleanup_backup` (no client or staff access).
- The coach's program editor (`StructureCanvas`, program-library_.$templateId.tsx: the template editor and a client's block editor, `/admin/blocks/$blockId`) is one sticky header and a list of days, on a phone and a computer alike. The header (`BuilderHeader`, program-editor/builder-chrome.tsx) is ← · the name typed in place (`BuilderTitleInput`) over one quiet line (client · status · weeks, or the template's kind) · Save only while something's unsaved, the save state only when it's news (saving, saved, failed, offline), Edit with AI ("AI" on a phone) and ⋯ (a client's profile and messages; a template's settings, which replace the canvas until Back to the program); its second row is the canvas's toolbar, portalled in (`toolbarTarget`, inline only in full screen): undo · redo · the weeks (`WeekNavSlotContext`: neutral numbered chips, + adds one; Week / Whole block on a computer) · ⋯ (1RM and training maxes for a client's block; the library, compact cards, full screen and zoom on a computer). A client's block is one block (`singleBlock`): no program / block strip (adding a block there never saved and archiving it would empty the block); a template keeps one row of block tabs. Under the header only what's news (`BuilderNotice`: a block that isn't running, workouts with no date, calendar issues), the client's blocks (`BlockSwitcher`, neutral), then the week: its line (name, phase, days, length) with the week's ⋯ (notes and phase, duplicate, Copy to weeks… onto the weeks picked in `WeekPickerSheet`, the member builder's picker, delete with Undo, never a confirm); an exercise's ⋯ has Progress across weeks… (`ProgressAcrossWeeksSheet`, program-editor/progress-across-weeks.tsx, in the shared `BuilderSheet`: every week of the block's sets, reps, RPE or RIR, load and rest for that exercise typed straight in, and a start + step pattern previewed before Fill, with Undo; read and written by the Sheet view's own `applyCell` / `progression` / `progressionText`, so a % keeps its basis and effort stops at 10), which is how a coach on a phone progresses a block without the Sheet view, Volume (`WeeklyVolumeSummary`, folded), priority checks, and the days, two or three across on a wide canvas (`@container`). A day is one card: "Day 1 · Wed, Nov 4 (`DayDateButton variant="inline"`, client blocks only) · Done · 50–60 min" with its ⋯ (open / close every exercise, paste, an empty row on a computer, a focus line, Copy to weeks… through the same picker, duplicate, delete with Undo), then the workout's name typed in place (what the athlete reads: `formatDaySubtitle`, saved to `subtitle` and `title`), its focus line, and the exercises as rows: a neutral number, the family stripe, the name, the prescription in one line ("3 × 8-12 · RPE 8 · tempo 1-0-1-2 · rest 1m 30s"), the label only when it isn't Assistance, an amber camera for no demo video, and ⋯ (edit, swap, duplicate, add back-off sets, copy, the demo, clear, delete; the next rule). An open row is Swap + the fields (sets · reps or time, its kind in the label's menu · RPE · RIR · rest; six across on a narrow card, twelve on a wide one, by the card's own width), Suggested load (a `Switch`, weight or % of a max), tempo, and More (label, movement family, exercise type); inputs are theme wells, never white boxes. Its fields are module-level (`RowField`): a component made inside the row remounted every input on each commit and threw the cursor out mid-typing. Focus moving into a layer the card opened (its ⋯, a select's list, a popover, the exercise picker: `inFloatingLayer`) never opens or folds it, and keys inside one are the layer's. Exercises are added and swapped through the searchable picker (`PhoneExercisePickerSheet`: a bottom sheet on a phone, the right side on a computer, with "Use a custom name"), never the old 2,000-item Select. The library both editors read is `useBuilderExerciseLibrary` (builder-exercise-library.ts: every unarchived exercise, page by page, one column set under one key) and purpose labels are read by each row's place in the week (`weekPurposeLabelsByDay`, program-editor-labels.ts). A client's Schedule, Athlete roadmap and Warm-ups fold under the program (`BuilderSection`, mounted when opened; `#roadmap` opens that one) — why: on a phone the client editor stacked nine bars and cards (a breadcrumb card, a status strip, a sticky bar, block pills, a tip, undo and a red Set 1RM button, a "PROGRAM · 1 active block · Add block" card, a block strip and the week's sticky bar) before the first exercise, every exercise was a big card with caps tags, a coloured number and white input boxes, the day header squeezed its name box to one letter, the library was one request the API cut at 1,000 of ~2,040 exercises (1,846 rows read "Unnamed exercise": Hip Thrust, Larsen Press…), the two editors shared its cache key with different columns ("Videos 0/6", "0 effective" sets), every row read "Assistance" because the labels were looked up by a row id the builder never has, and the day's real name (its `title`, "Lower A (Squat Focus)") never showed.
- The coach's program editor moves, copies and progresses exercises the way the member builder does (Nov 23 2026, program-editor-rows.ts pure and tested in program-editor-rows.test.ts, program-editor/sortable-rows.tsx): a day's rows are exercises, a row plus the back-offs after it (`rowUnits` / `followsHead`: the same exercise at a % of the top set or the previous set, or one the coach calls a back-off, "Primary Squat Backoff": the logger's and pl_day_set_names' own reading), dragged, copied, swapped and removed together. Every exercise has a handle (`RowGrip`, always there, a 40px tap on a phone; a tap or a hold on it never opens the card, `onGrip`): a mouse drags at once, a finger presses and holds it (TouchSensor delay 160, so a swipe anywhere still scrolls), Space and the arrows for a keyboard, the exercise lifting and the others sliding aside, the page scrolling at its edges, haptics on pick-up, each pass and the drop (`SortableUnits` / `SortableUnit`); there's no Move up / Move down and no browser drag of rows. The row's ⋯ has Duplicate (the exercise with its back-offs right under it, `freshCopy`: no saved id, so it's inserted, never an update of the original, its set blocks cloned through `_sourceDbId` like Paste; a back-off's "Duplicate this back-off" copies just it), Add back-off sets (two sets at 90% of the top set, its reps, rest and tempo, labelled from its role, `backoffRow` / `backoffLabelFor`; a second group copies the last; only on an exercise lifted for reps with a weight) and "Delete with its 2 back-offs"; a swap takes the back-offs along with every number kept. A back-off sits under its top set with a corner instead of a number (exercises are numbered by exercise, `position`), titled Back-off / Back-off 2, and says what it comes to from the top set's prescribed weight ("90% of top set = 350 lb", `backoffLoad`, rounded like the logger's `backoffRoundStep`; an RPE top set has no number until it's lifted, so it says it's worked out from that day's top set), with Round to (5 lb · 2.5 kg or 2.5 lb · 1 kg, `load_round`, saved by the block editor's `ROW_FIELDS` and copied from a template's rows on assign by `pl_apply_template_meta`, 20270510090000_template_load_round.sql). An edit changes its week only (the week view says "Edits change week 3 only"); its toast says so and offers the same edit in the later weeks laid out the same (`laidOutTheSame`: that day with the same exercises in the same order, never an earlier week and never a workout trained or started), each keeping its own numbers (`replayRowOp`), with Undo for exactly those weeks' day; a later edit with nothing to offer clears an earlier offer. A row's key lives on the row (`_k`, `rowKeysOf` / `stampRowKeys`), since the editor copies its payload on every change, and is never saved (`withoutRowKeys` in the template's persist; the block editor saves only `ROW_FIELDS`) — why: on Nov 22 2026 the coach asked for a fast, intuitive builder (smooth drag with a handle, mobile press-and-hold, quick duplicates, top sets and back-offs without duplicating whole exercises, obvious week scope), and after the member builder got it, "Continue until complete" brought it to his own editor, where rows dragged only on a computer by a hover-only handle, Move up / Move down sat in every ⋯, back-offs were look-alike exercises with nothing tying them to their top set, and a change in one week had to be redone week by week or copied over whole weeks.
- Anything in Crew can be reported (`community_report`: a post, comment, program or account the reporter can see, never their own; `ReportSheet`, the post ⋯ → Report for everyone but staff) into `community_reports` (staff read only; who reported is never shown, not even to staff in the review). The coach's Crew → Feed "Needs you" leads with the reports (`useOpenReports`, `ReportsReviewSheet`, the Feed tab's dot counts them), and a report pushes every admin and coach (`notifyCommunityReport`, called by `ReportSheet` after the report saves: only someone with an open report on that thing can fire it, once per reported thing per staff login, at most one every 30 minutes, content-free and never who reported): Remove archives a post (its author keeps it), hides a comment or takes a program down; Dismiss leaves it; three people (accounts at least 7 days old, never staff) reporting a program take it down until staff look; putting a program back up (`athlete_program_moderate`) settles its open reports, re-sending a settled report never reopens it, and Duplicate keeps a taken-down program down — why: Crew opened to anyone who signs up, and a community open to the public needs a way to flag abuse (the App Store requires it for user content, and that reports are acted on promptly) that doesn't depend on the coach seeing every post.
- A "what's new" popup (`ThemeAnnouncementGate` and any like it) shows only to accounts made before the thing it announces (`isNewsTo(user.created_at, …_NEW_SINCE)`, whats-new.ts); someone who joined since has had it from day one — why: a brand-new free member's first minutes stacked the training setup, "Pounds or kilograms?", Add to Home Screen and "NEW: Dark mode is here", one after another.
- A test login (an address on a reserved test domain: example.com / .org / .net, or ending .test, .example, .invalid, .localhost; `public.is_test_login`) never shows to the crew: it's left out of `community_member_directory` (Find athletes, suggestions, the @ picker, mentions) and its post views are dropped (`community_post_views_skip_test_logins`, 20270203090000_test_logins_out_of_crew.sql). Testing on the live app signs in only with such logins and deletes them when done — why: on Nov 5 a QA run's "QA Tester A / B" (jf.qa.member.a/b@example.com) showed to every client as "New to the Crew" under Suggested for you, and in the views on the coach's lesson.
- Live challenges (20270206090000_live_challenges.sql, `src/lib/challenges/`, `/challenges*`, `/athletes/$handle`, `/admin/challenges*`) are public pages fed only by SECURITY DEFINER reads (`challenge_hub` / `challenge_public` / `challenge_board` / `challenge_entry_public` / `challenge_athlete`) that return public fields: contact details live in `challenge_people_private` (admins, and the person once claimed), bodyweight is used only to break ties and never leaves staff screens, and no challenge table is readable by anon except `challenge_live` (a version counter realtime pushes; pages refetch on it and poll as a backup). Scores are written only by `challenge_record_attempt` / `_correct_attempt` / `_void_attempt` (judge, manager or admin of that challenge via `challenge_staff`), idempotent on the screen's `client_key`, every change in `challenge_audit`, a correction voiding the old score with its reason; a challenge's status, start/end and final results change only through `challenge_set_status` / `challenge_finalize` (`challenge_guard`), a draft can't publish without a date, venue and rules, and only drafts, templates and test challenges can be deleted. Ranking is one function (`_challenge_rank`): best or summed verified attempts, per bodyweight when relative, then the published tie-break only when everyone in that tie has its value (lighter bodyweight or a run-off), else they share the place; submission order never breaks a tie, and a division with its own load is its own board. A public sign-up never attaches to an existing profile by typed email (staff registration and a signed-in person's confirmed email do; `challenge_claim` merges by confirmed email); photos need consent and a one-time ticket redeemed by `uploadChallengePhoto` on the server. Time text on these pages renders only after mount (`useMounted`): the server and a phone can disagree on Manitoba's rules and the clock — why: anyone can open a challenge link, judges work on bad gym wifi, a tie or a typo must never silently decide a prize, and contact details must never reach a leaderboard, card or judge.
- A challenge's page is leaderboard first (20270208090000_challenges_leaderboard_first.sql, `board.ts`, `leaderboard.tsx`): a compact header with one main button (`primaryAction`: Register / Find my ranking / You're registered / View rankings / View final results), then the board, then the details folded into an accordion. The board is paged by the database (`challenge_board_page`, `useBoardPages`, both directions from where you jump to), never the whole board on the phone; search reads the whole event (`challenge_board_find`: name or bib, capitals, accents, one wrong or swapped letter forgiven, registered-but-unscored athletes included, the bib shown so two people with one name can be told apart) and "find my ranking" (`challenge_board_around`: place, the athletes around, the next place up and the gap in words, `standingLine`) works only from the viewer's own entry (`challenge_public.my_code` for a signed-in athlete, else the one registered on this phone), never from a name they typed. Who's on the platform and the judge's count so far live in `challenge_now` (one row per challenge, realtime, shown only while fresh, `isFresh`), set by a judge's Start → count → Done → Confirm (`challenge_set_now`, `challenge_clear_now` only ever clears that judge's own athlete) and shown on the page and the big screen as in progress, never as a result. The builder is four autosaved steps (`/admin/challenges/$id/edit`, `challenge-wizard.tsx`, `wizard.ts`: a draft exists the moment its kind is picked, untitled until named; `buildPatch` sends only what changed and holds back an invalid value while the rest saves); once a published challenge has a verified score its scoring (measure, direction, unit, attempts, aggregate, per-bodyweight, tie rule) is locked by `challenge_guard`. The organizer's page is a dashboard (`challenge_dashboard`: counts, leader, recent activity from `challenge_audit`) with the next step for where the event is, and self sign-up follows `registration_opens_at` / `registration_closes_at` (`challenge_register`, `challenge_public.registration_open`) — why: the first version stacked a hero, eight blocks and a share panel above a board that only searched the rows already loaded, the builder was one long form saved only at the end, and a big-screen audience had no idea who was lifting.
- Getting a client into the app is one link and one tap: every setup link (Text, Copy, Email, and the auto-send on Add client) opens `/setup?invite=<token>` (setup-invite.ts, signed by setup-invite.server.ts, `SETUP_INVITE_DAYS` = 14, dead once they've signed in), and an emailed one also carries its one-time token (`scannerSafeAuthLink` keeps `invite` on /setup), so a spent or expired token falls back to the invite (`autoContinue`) instead of "expired". The setup page (`SetupInviteStep`) starts on one of three steps (`setupInviteState`): `continue` (an email the coach entered: Continue mints a sign-in, `mintSetupToken`, and they pick one password), `email` (none on file: they type theirs, it must be new to JF Effect, and the invite email carries a 6-digit code), `code` (a typed email waiting on its code: `verifySetupCode`, Send a new code, Use a different email); an email the client typed signs in only by its code or link (`setup_self_entered_email` on the login until confirmed), never by Continue. After the password comes the Coaching Agreement (or later) and then the app; no socials step. A run-out link asks for their email and sends a fresh one (`requestNewSetupLink`, the same answer either way). Text is the coach's first button. The client profile (`admin/clients.$id`) saves only what the coach changed (`profileChanges`, client-profile-form.ts), never its copy of the whole row; a refetch moves every field the coach hasn't touched (`rebaseProfileForm`), and `PROFILE_READ_ONLY_FIELDS` (the GENERATED `normalized_email` / `normalized_phone`, sex, the schedule) are never written. A client's assigned coach is read on its own (`coachById` / `assignedCoachOf` / `withAssignedCoach` / `isAssignedCoachOf`, sms-identity.ts), never embedded: `clients.assigned_coach_id` has no foreign key to `coaches`. The setup buttons throw on failure so they never read "Sent" / "Texted" over an error — why: Devin's Text setup link failed with "Could not find a relationship between 'clients' and 'coaches'" (every text that named the coach, the evening-before session reminders included, failed the same way), the profile's Save failed on every client with "column "normalized_email" can only be updated to DEFAULT" (and would have unlinked a login a setup link had just made), links died after 24 hours on a dead-end screen, a client with no email couldn't be invited at all, and the coach wants people into their account fast: one link, one tap, one password.
- Training PDF (the Clients list ⋯, the client's Training tab ⋯, the athlete's own Workouts ⋯; until Nov 22 2026 "Download Training Report") is one sheet, `TrainingReportSheet` (training-report-sheet.tsx), Program (what to do: every workout of the blocks picked, current and upcoming ticked, a coach also offered a hidden draft) or Report (what was done against what was prescribed, started blocks ticked), drawn by the PDF system (the next rule): the blocks newest first (what's running and done first, not-started ones after), each with its dates and "N of M done", All blocks one tap, what has started or been trained ticked by default (`defaultReportSelection`), one PDF of the picked blocks oldest first. Its data is read once when the sheet opens by `loadTrainingReportSource` (training-report-data.ts): blocks / weeks / days / exercise rows through `.in()` chunks of 150, logged sets, completions and exercise notes by `client_id`, every read paged past 1,000 rows; never one `.in()` over every row, and never written to the phone (`training-report-` is in query-persister's `DO_NOT_PERSIST_PREFIXES`). A day's session is its finished calendar instance (`pickCompletion`) and its sets that instance's (`setsForSession`); archived, hidden and at-home backup blocks and removed days show only what was trained; a coach's copy carries their private day notes, a client's only the ones marked for them. The PDF (compressed) is made while the sheet is open and handed over in the tap through `saveFiles` (file-share.ts: the share sheet on a phone, "again" asks for one more tap, a download on a computer) — why: the old download asked for every logged set in one request (Jared's 1,124 row ids, a ~44 KB URL the server refused), so it failed with "Could not generate training report." for any client with a few blocks, a client with more than 1,000 sets would have lost the rest silently, `doc.save()` does nothing in the iPhone app's web view, and the coach wanted to pick blocks instead of always getting all of them.
- Every training, nutrition and report PDF is one design system (Nov 22 2026, src/lib/pdf: kit.ts, charts.ts, text.ts, deliver.ts; pdf-system.test.ts): US Letter, portrait unless a document's tables need the width (`orientationFor`: Program Intelligence is landscape), the PDF standard Helvetica (no font to embed, every word searchable; `pdfText` turns what it can't draw into words: → "to", ≥ ">=", − "-", emoji dropped, while — – • · × … print as themselves), a cover (logo, what it is in the brand red, the title, a row of facts), a running header on every later page, "Page N of M" on every page, neutral greys, hairlines instead of boxes, green done, amber attention, red missed, nothing under 7.5 pt. Tables (`PdfDoc.table`) are measured on a scratch page and kept whole on one when they fit, else broken between rows with their headers repeated and their title row saying "(continued)" (or `atPageTop`, "Week 2 · Day 3 · Pull"); columns fit their content (`fit`), shrink in proportion when they'd overflow, and the widest stretches when none is flexible; a muted second line under a cell (`sub`: an exercise's role), notes under a row and footnotes under a table. A heading is never drawn alone: it waits (`pending`) and goes on the page with what follows it (`beforeContent`), a heading that starts a page splitting the run. Charts are vectors from the very numbers beside them (`drawLineChart`, `drawBarChart`). Every PDF reaches the person through `deliverPdf` / `makeAndDeliverPdf` (`saveFiles`: the share sheet on a phone, a download on a computer; a toast with Save when the phone won't open the sheet from the first tap), never jsPDF's `save()`, and is named `pdfFileName` (who, what, which part, the day: "jared-mcintyre-training-program-block-2-2026-11-22.pdf"). The documents: the Training program (workout-pdf.ts `generateProgramPdf`: blocks, the maxes a % reads when one does, "How to read it", each block's workouts as tables whose columns line up across the block, load as the coach wrote it ("75% TM", "92.5% of top set", a fixed load in its own `load_unit`), a week identical to the one before said in a line, the client's copy notes through `exerciseNoteForAthlete` and only day notes marked for them); the Training report (`buildTrainingReportDoc`: workouts done of those due, missed, sets logged, a chart of sets a week, then every trained workout as exercise · prescribed · logged ("Sets 2–4: 130 kg × 4 · RPE 7", as typed), the rest by status, `dayStatus` against today: Completed, In progress, Partly logged, Missed, Skipped (optional), Today, Upcoming); a JF Effect library plan (library-plan-pdf.ts, the same Program PDF made on the phone from the plan on screen; `downloadLibraryPdf` only checks and records it); the Nutrition plan (meal-plan-pdf.ts: the period and next update, each day type's targets and when it's used (`planDayWhen`), the coach's notes, supplements, weighing rules, the disclaimer, then each day type's meals with their macros and calories from them; never days averaged together: the coach's plan ⋯ Download PDF, the client's and a member's plan all print this one); the Grocery list; the Nutrition roadmap (roadmap-pdf.ts: what the person's own roadmap screen shows, the athlete's in their words, the coach's with his decision); the Progress report (analytics page's Report: progress-report-pdf.ts over the page's own training load and records for the period on screen, plus weigh-ins, the plan and the check-ins' nutrition ratings read with the viewer's login, the strength charts drawn from `LiftLoad.points`, the points the trend reads); the Block review (block-pdf.ts); Program Intelligence (program-intel/pdf.ts); a sale's Purchase record (purchase-pdf.ts). A source with nothing in it says so in a line, never a made-up number. The accountant's exports (books-export.ts) keep their own layout — why: on Nov 22 2026 the coach asked for one premium, accurate PDF system for training and nutrition; there were 11 generators in 4 styles on 2 paper sizes, 6 downloads used `doc.save()` (nothing happens in the iPhone app), the coach's nutrition "Download PDF" averaged training and rest days into one set of targets, the program PDF was dead code so nobody could print a program, future workouts read "NOT COMPLETED", "1   140 kg" read as 1140 kg, Program Intelligence printed → as garbage, tables were cut across pages with headings left behind, and the roadmap and the analytics had no PDF at all.
- What kind of account a client record is shows as one label read from the record, never a hand-set status (`clientAccountLabel`, client-account-label.ts): Lead (a coaching applicant who hasn't signed in), Client, Member (self-serve), Paused, Cancelling, Deactivated, Archived; every older status ("New Client", "Needs Attention", "Payment Overdue"…) reads as Client. The profile header, the Clients overlay's header and Goals' Coaching setup show it; Coaching setup's status picker offers only what the data can't tell (Lead, Client = `Active`, Paused, Cancelling; `statusChoicesFor` keeps an older value visible, never blank), and Deactivate / Archive stay their own actions. A coaching Lead becomes a client on their first sign-in (`mark_client_signed_in`, 20270223090000_lead_becomes_client_on_sign_in.sql: status `Active`, lifecycle `active_client`, `converted_to_client_at`, their open application `Approved`), since a coaching Lead can only have a login from the coach's setup link; the function writes as the server (claims cleared, then put back, like `_member_free_join`) because the clients self-update guards refuse a client changing `status` / `account_status` on their own row — why: Devin signed in from the coach's link and trained, yet stayed "Lead" (left out of every job that reads Active / New Client, and blank in the status picker, which had no Lead), 9 long-time clients still read "New Client", and the first sign-in after an emailed invite ('Invite Sent') raised in the guards, so it was never recorded.
- A set's row in the logger is its number, its boxes and (a coaching client's only) its video: there is no status column. The number is the done button (`SetNumberBadge`: open is a neutral grey circle, `bg-muted`, done a filled green one in the theme's `success` green, a saving spinner or a red error in its place), tapping it runs the same `saveCompletionStatus` the old check did, so typed values still complete a set by themselves exactly as before; a done row is only a whisper of green (`bg-success/[0.04]`, no left bar: the number says it). The columns picker is the header's first cell, and taking one set out is a swipe left on it or the card's ⋯ (the set-removal rule below). Every row, header and warm-up row takes its columns from `setGridTemplate` (set-grid.ts). A set's PRs are one small gold trophy chip right under it (`SetPrIndicator`, `setPrItems` in set-pr-items.ts: the server's rep and weight records for the set at their top scope, else, where the logger has no records or the set is assisted, the on-phone rep-max check; nothing new is detected): one record shows its name ("5-rep ATPR"), more show "2 PRs", and a tap opens each with what it beat ("145 kg × 5 · was 140 kg", from `prev_*_kg`); it pops in once when just earned (`prFresh`, 2 minutes) beside the PR sound and haptic the row already plays — why: a 44px status column (a big check plus a −) took about a third of each row on a phone and squeezed the weight, reps and RPE boxes, and the coach found the strong green rows and the big solid-orange caps PR pills under sets visually overwhelming.
- Taking a set out of a workout is Instagram's swipe, and never deletes the coach's prescription (Nov 20 2026, set-session.ts pure and tested, swipe-to-reveal.tsx `variant="row"`): a swipe left on a set or warm-up row shows one small action flush with the row, Delete (red) for a set the athlete added with + Add set or a warm-up, Skip (neutral) for one of the program's sets; there's no trash icon on any row, and the card's ⋯ has the same for anyone who doesn't swipe ("Skip a set today (stays in your program)" `card-skip-set`, "Delete a set you added" `card-delete-set`; a warm-up's flame menu). Which sets were added is remembered on the phone per workout (`getSetSession` / `setSetSession`: `planned`, the row's `pl_exercise_rows.sets` before the first + Add set there; every set past it is added), and a phone that never saw the add treats every set as the program's (Skip, the safe side). Delete lowers the row's `sets`; Skip never writes the row: it hides that set for today (`skipped`, `shownSetCount`), the prescription stays, so the recap's Vs the plan, Program Intelligence and the coach still read it as planned and not done, while the workout's ring, Focus bars and finish review count it as nothing left to do (`rowsWithSkips` on the rows `workoutSetProgress` reads); + Add set brings a skipped set back first (`afterAdd`, nothing written), and a skip never hides a set with numbers on it (`withLoggedShown`: an undo from the ⋯ or another phone logging it brings it back). Either way the set's logged numbers go and the later sets move up a number by row id, lowest first (`takeOutWrites`; pl_row_results is unique per client, row and set number, and a set's video follows its row), shown at once; there's no confirm, the toast's Undo puts it back exactly (`putSetBack`: the rows move back highest first, `putBackWrites`, the removed row returns with its values, the row's count and the skip are restored). Warm-ups (pl_warmup_sets) are never work, so deleting one touches no set, adherence or analytic; a logged one still read for today's weight just stops being read. The swipe is the card's own swipe (the exercise card's Delete steps aside when a gesture starts inside a nested swipe, `[data-swipe-root]`), locked to a clearly sideways drag past 24px, `touch-pan-y` so a vertical drag from any row or box scrolls, never from a box being typed in, and in Focus a quick swipe left still moves to the next exercise (it asks Focus on release like the card does); checked with real touch events in Chromium (sideways opens, vertical scrolls, a tap on a box still types, the card's header keeps the card's Delete) — why: on Nov 20 2026 the coach wanted adding and removing sets equally easy and clean on a phone: removing was a grid of numbers in the ⋯ behind a browser confirm that deleted the set's numbers and lowered the coach's prescription for that workout (a skipped set read as a fully done 3-set exercise), and a warm-up could only be removed from its flame's menu.
- Set videos: a coaching client's logged set can carry videos for their coach (`useEntitlement("lift_review")`, never a member; `pl_set_videos`, 20270302090000_set_videos.sql). A warm-up can too (20270329090000_warmup_videos.sql): its clip is set 0 on the exercise row, `warmup_id` its pl_warmup_sets row (never a working set's `result_id`, a check holds both), passed to `set_video_attach` in `_result_id` when `_set_index` is 0 (no new signature), named "Warm-up" in the chat, tied to a warm-up saved after it was filmed (`pl_warmup_sets_link_set_videos`, 18 hours), cleared of its warm-up when moved onto a set; `videoOnSet` / `jobOnSet` never put a warm-up's clip on a set or a set's on a warm-up, `setWord` / `setTitle` (label.ts) say it, and it's never in the workout's film, a feed copy or the catch-up (set_index > 0 there). The set's video cell (`SetVideoCell`, set-video-cell.tsx) is a quiet camera on every set, logged or not (the app's own camera, below, with Library for the phone's picker; people film a set before they log it: a clip filmed before the set is saved goes up with no set record and is on the set by its exercise row and set number, `videoOnSet` / `jobOnSet` in set-videos/jobs.ts, only within `UNLINKED_SET_VIDEO_MS` so a repeat of the workout never shows an old one, and the database ties it to the set's record as soon as there is one: saving the set links it, `pl_row_results_link_set_videos`, and `set_video_attach` finds the record itself when the set was saved mid-upload, 20270308090000_set_video_before_logging.sql; its label is sent only once the set is logged, since an unlogged set's boxes can hold its targets), a progress ring while it sends, the clip's poster once sent (a tap opens `SetVideoSheet`: play it, open the chat, remove it, add another), a cloud while it waits for a connection and red only when it needs a tap. `set_video_attach` (SECURITY DEFINER, the only writer) records it and posts one coach-chat message in the same transaction (`message_type` 'Set Video', a `type: "video"` attachment carrying `set_video` {exercise, set, label}, its words "Exercise · Top set · 100 kg × 5 @ RPE 8", never an emoji: the set by its name in the workout, `pl_set_name`, then `setVideoLabel`), idempotent on the job's `client_key` and on `(client_id, file_key)` (the picked file hashed with the client: the same clip twice is one upload and one message), and refuses a video the server didn't check (`media_video_allowed`), a path outside `<client>/set-videos/`, a set that isn't this client's and an account with no coach; `set_video_remove` takes it off the set and deletes its message for everyone, and unsending or deleting the message takes it off the set. A clip on the wrong set moves from its ⋯ in the set's sheet (Save to Photos stays in view; the ⋯ holds Move to another set and Remove, nothing more): `SetVideoMovePicker` (set-video-move.tsx) lists the workout's exercises (one on two cards by its role there, Focus's `focusNames`: "Competition Squat · Backoff") with their warm-ups first (a flame, numbered when there are several, `warmupChipNumber`, each logged warm-up with its numbers, else the warm-up still to log on a lift that takes one, `takesWarmup`: never cardio done for time or a lift done with no weight) and then their set numbers (`moveChoices`, set-videos/move.ts: as many as written or logged; the one it's on filled, logged ones solid, the rest dashed; a clip older than `UNLINKED_SET_VIDEO_MS` goes only onto a logged one, since only a fresh clip can wait on a set), one tap moves it with Undo (back onto the warm-up it came from too), and `set_video_move` (20270310090000_set_video_move.sql, warm-ups 20270420090000_set_video_move_warmups.sql: the athlete, the sender, their coach or an admin, within the same workout only; set 0 with `_warmup_id` a logged warm-up of that exercise and athlete, without it the one still to log, tied when it's saved; older apps still call it with five arguments) moves the clip onto that set's or warm-up's record (never both), relabels its chat message and the attachment's set details as the server (claims cleared and put back; a client can't change an attachment on their own message; a warm-up's card says Warm-up, `warmup: true`) and its caption on the workout's film (a clip moved onto a warm-up leaves the film), the label being the set's own numbers (`filmLabels(filmSetValues(…))`, a warm-up's `describeWarmup`); why the warm-ups: on Nov 20 2026 the coach put a clip on the wrong exercise and the picker had no warm-ups to move it to, and an exercise on two cards read the same twice. Who sees it is the coach chat's own rule: the file is in `message-attachments` under the client's folder and the table is SELECT-only for the client, their coach (`coached_client_ids()`) and admins. The coach reviews and answers in the chat like any message (push "sent you a set video", inbox detail "Set video"). The camera is pointed out: the moment a coaching client logs a set (never on sets logged before the workout opened), a bubble on that set's camera says "Film your sets for {coach}" with Film this set (the phone's picker, from that tap) and Not now (`set-videos/tip.ts`, `useSetVideoTip` → `setVideoTipDue`): one bubble per app open (`claimSetVideoTip`, handed on when it couldn't show), only once the person isn't typing and no sheet is up, at most once every `SET_VIDEO_TIP_GAP_MS` and `SET_VIDEO_TIP_MAX` days, counted on the account (`workout_view_prefs.set_video_tip_count` / `_last_at` through `workout_view_set_video_tip_shown`, 20270306090000_set_video_tip.sql; never the phone), never once they've sent a set video and never to a coach viewing as the client; a read that fails shows nothing — why: the camera is quiet on purpose and clients didn't know a set could be filmed and sent, and the coach wants them to start; and on Nov 12 2026 Jared's lateral raise, filmed before he'd logged it, had no camera at all (it showed only on logged sets). Sending (`src/lib/set-videos/uploader.ts`, loaded on demand and warmed in the background once someone is logging, so a pick still starts with no signal; the flow and the sheet render only once loaded, never through `React.lazy`, whose failure box would sit inside the set row) opens a quick look first (the trimmer with the lift found, Send: the lift rule above; backing out leaves nothing on the set), then runs one clip at a time through the shared video engine (`planVideo` / `renderVideo`: a small or lean clip goes as it is, a heavy one is re-encoded to 720p only when that beats sending it, `encodeTooSlow` falls back), shows on the set at once, uploads resumably (`uploadLiftFileToStorage` `resume: true`, TUS over 6 MB), has the server read it back (`verifyVideoUploadFn`) and then attaches. The prepared clip and each finished step are kept on the phone (`outbox.ts`, IndexedDB, merged in one transaction, 7 days, cleared on sign-out), so an app iOS closed carries on from the bytes that landed the next time it opens (`SetVideoResume`); a dropped connection retries with backoff and at once when the phone is back online or on screen, and the logger never waits on any of it — why: the coach wants to see a specific set with its weight, reps and RPE without the athlete leaving the logger to write a message, gym connections drop and iOS closes backgrounded home-screen apps, and a double tap or the same clip picked twice must never become two uploads or two messages.
- Filming in the app is the app's own camera, never iOS's "Take Video" (`FilmCamera`, components/camera/film-camera.tsx; film-settings.ts pure and tested): a web page's file picker records at 480 × 360 under 1 Mbps with no settings (every set video filmed that way through Nov 2026 came in at 360 × 480, while the same phones' Photos gave 4K). The set's camera, its tip's Film this set and Add another open it (`openCamera` in set-video-cell.tsx, loaded with the rest of the video code; a phone without a live camera or a recorder, `canFilmInApp`, gets its own picker from the same tap); it films the live camera (getUserMedia + MediaRecorder) at 1080p30 by default, or 720p / 4K and 60 fps where the camera says it can (`availableQualities` / `availableFps` from `getCapabilities`, asked as ideal values with the shape, `constraintLadder`, and the chip says what it's really getting, `describeTrack`), full screen like TikTok's and a reel's: the picture edge to edge (`object-cover`), the controls over it, and a picture that fills a phone's screen, 9:16 held upright (`pictureFits`): browsers read the size asked for against different ways round, so the camera checks the picture it got and, when it's 3:4 or sideways, asks with the size written the other way (`askOrder`, `AskShape`), keeps the closer of the two when neither is 9:16 (never no camera for the shape) and remembers what worked on the phone (`jf-film-camera-ask-v1`); while it's up the status bar and everything behind it are black (`holdCameraOpen` paints `theme-color` and `html[data-camera-open]`, styles.css), never the page's light grey over the top of the picture or under its foot, at a clean bitrate sized for Crew (`recorderBitrate`: ~6 Mbps 1080p30, so a set of a minute fits a workout post's 50 MB whole and goes into the film with no copy to wait for; H.264 MP4 at the level the picture needs, `recorderTypes`, WebM on Android), with the iPhone's lenses by name (`lensesFrom`: 0.5 · 1× · Tele, never the combined Dual / Triple cameras), pinch to zoom and the light where the camera has them, the front camera, a grid, and Record sound (in the camera's settings, shared with the Crew studio's hold-to-film, `useClipRecorder` `withSound`, read when a clip starts): it's off on a phone or tablet until someone switches it on (`defaultFilmSound`), so filming never opens the mic and the music in their earbuds keeps playing with nothing to tap, since a page's open mic puts iOS in PlayAndRecord (which can't mix) and Chrome on Android takes audio focus, and no page can change that; on, the mic is asked for only when filming starts (`openVideoMic`) and pauses their music; saves from before Nov 21 2026 count only a sound-off as a choice (`storedSoundChoice`, saves now carry `audio`); a clip with no sound track never takes the page's "playback" session when watched back (`installMediaPlaybackSession` skips `clipIsSilent`, waiting for the header when played before it), so reviewing one doesn't pause the music either; under the exercise and set the screen says the set to lift in big type (`detail`, `data-film-detail`, kept on screen while it films: `SetVideoCell` `aim`, the weight in its box, else the one a tap on its number logs, with the reps and RPE on it, "140 kg × 3 @ RPE 7"; the coach's chat label stays the logged set only); the settings are remembered on the phone (`jf-film-camera-v1`, cameras differ phone to phone). One tap starts, one stops (a propped phone films the whole set), the screen stays awake while it films, it stops before memory runs out (`recordLimitSeconds`, `SET_RECORD_MAX_SECONDS` 5 min, about 2 at 4K), the system ending the camera keeps what was filmed, and the clip goes on exactly like a picked one (`onPicked`: the quick look, the lift finder, the upload; named `jf-film-…` so it's offered to Photos). Library opens the phone's picker inside its own tap. While it's up the phone may turn on its side to film (`holdCameraOpen`, camera-presence.ts: Android's lock is let go until it closes, `PortraitLock`). The Crew studio's hold-to-film records at the same bitrate rule and asks for the mic through `getMicStream` (asked directly on an "ambient" page, WebKit refused it and clips came out silent) — why: on Nov 17 2026 the coach filmed a set in the app and it came out blurry with no camera settings; and on Nov 18 2026 that it didn't film to the full screen, where he wanted it to fill the screen like a reel or TikTok; and on Nov 21 2026 that clients filming their sets want their music to keep playing in their earbuds, and it stopped every time (sound was on by default, and the only way to keep music was a switch buried in the camera's settings); a Music / Sound button on the camera was the first try, and the coach wanted it to just happen with nothing to tap; and that the filming screen named only the set ("2-Count Pause Bench Press · Set 1"), where the athlete needs to see the weight and reps they're about to lift.
- A video filmed in the app records the room as it sounds, from the device's own microphone, the way the phone's camera does (Nov 19 2026, video-mic.ts, `openVideoMic`): every in-app video recorder (the set camera `FilmCamera`, the Crew studio's hold-to-film `useClipRecorder`) asks for the mic through `videoAudioConstraints`, which turns off each call process the browser supports (`echoCancellation`, `noiseSuppression`, `autoGainControl`, `voiceIsolation` set false) and asks for stereo and 48 kHz as `ideal`, never a hard requirement, only the switches `getSupportedConstraints` lists (Safari lists only echo cancellation and sample rate; an unknown browser gets just `echoCancellation: false`). On iOS `echoCancellation` is the whole difference: WebKit captures through Apple's VoiceProcessingIO (VoIP echo cancelling, noise suppression and gain riding, ducking other audio) while it's on and the plain RemoteIO mic when it's off (CoreAudioCaptureUnit.cpp). A Bluetooth hands-free mic (`isBluetoothMic`: AirPods, Beats, Buds, Bluetooth, hands-free) is never what a video records from when the device's own is there (`micToName` before opening: Chrome's `default` entry, or any Bluetooth input on Safari, where iOS routes a hands-free mic the moment the mic opens; `pickOwnMic` after opening when there were no labels yet, the headset's stream stopped and the own mic named `exact`). Anything but a permission refusal falls back: without the device named, then the plain `getMicStream(true)` the app always used, so a video never loses its sound to this. Each clip filmed in the app notes what its sound came from (`micNote`: "raw 2ch 48k own", "voice 1ch 48k bt fallback", never a device's name; `noteClipMic` by file name), and a set video's upload carries it in `pl_set_videos.transfer.mic`. Voice memos and Cleo's dictation keep the call processing and the headset (speech into a mic is what it's for). Progress videos (`AngleUploadCard`) and a chat's + menu Video (coach chat and group / 1:1 chats, `ComposerPlusMenu onPickVideo`) film in the same camera (`useFilmCamera`, use-film-camera.tsx: `start()` opens it or says false where a page can't film, so the caller opens its picker in the same tap; Library inside it hands over to the caller's picker); the Camera tile stays the phone's own (photos and videos). The camera's root is `pointer-events-auto`, so it takes its taps over a modal it was opened from (a progress video's dialog), whose lock leaves the rest of the page untappable; rendered from inside the dialog's React tree, its taps never count as outside it — why: on Nov 19 2026 the coach found in-app recordings sometimes muffled, compressed, distorted or unnatural next to the phone's own camera; every recorder asked for the mic with the browser's defaults, which are a call's, and a test clip through the real FilmCamera in Chromium measured +15–18 dB of gain riding, a −3 dBFS bar set-down clipped to 0 dBFS, a quiet room pumped up 18 dB, a 10 dB wander in level and a waveform no longer close to what the mic heard (fidelity −20 dB), against 0 dB, −2.96 dBFS, 1.4 dB and 23 dB (the codec's own limit) with the processing off (video-mic.test.ts); with AirPods connected WebKit's PlayAndRecord session (the hands-free option on) recorded the AirPods' narrowband call mic, the "sometimes"; and 9 videos in the 45 days to Nov 19 came from iOS's "Take Video" picker (chat's Camera, a fallback) at 480 × 360 and 0.86 Mbps, which a web page can't configure. Still iOS's while the mic is open, out of a web page's reach: WebKit forces the session to PlayAndRecord in VideoChat mode (voice-tuned equalization, music from other apps stops, a mono capture), so an in-app clip on an iPhone is cleaner than before but not the Camera app's stereo.
- Filming asks for the camera and the mic inside the tap that needs them, so an "Allow" already given is used without asking again (Nov 20 2026): the tap that opens the film camera starts its first camera request itself (`primeCamera`, camera-prime.ts: the same first ask the camera makes, `firstCameraAsk`; the Crew camera's + tap does the same with the studio's own first ask, `primeCamera(studioCameraAsk("environment"))` in share-workout-picker's `begin`, which the studio takes before asking for its own, Nov 21 2026; the camera takes that answer on its first ask, `takePrimedCamera`, an answer for anything else is let go before it asks for its own, and one nobody takes is stopped after `PRIME_TTL_MS`), and the record tap's mic request goes out before anything is awaited (`openVideoMic`: the device to name comes from the list kept from the last recording, `knownInputs`, refreshed after each grant and on `devicechange`, never listed first). A "Don't Allow" is remembered for the visit (`micRefused`): the set camera and the Crew studio film without sound and never ask on every clip, and the camera's settings say so with Ask again (`askForMicAgain`: asked inside that tap and let go at once, so nothing records and the music carries on). The mic is still opened only for a recording and stopped right after it. What a page can't change: WebKit forgets every camera and mic "Allow" 10 minutes after capture stops (`inactiveMediaCaptureStreamRepromptIntervalInMinutes`, every platform), and on an iPhone a request that isn't from a tap is asked about again once nothing has been captured for a minute (`inactiveMediaCaptureStreamRepromptWithoutUserGestureIntervalInMinutes`, 1 on iOS, 10 elsewhere); Safari's own Website Settings (Camera / Microphone: Allow) and the iPhone app, whose web view grants capture itself (Capacitor's `requestMediaCapturePermissionFor` → `.grant`; only iOS's one-time system prompt), never ask again. With sound off by default on a phone (the filming rule above) the microphone is never asked for at all, so filming in Safari or the home-screen app is one camera question at most per launch, and none while the camera was used in the last 10 minutes. In development the camera opens twice (TanStack Start's client entry wraps the app in StrictMode), so only the first open takes the tap's answer there — why: on Nov 20 2026 the coach had to tap "Allow" for the microphone every time he filmed a set: the mic was asked for only after `await enumerateDevices()` and the camera from an effect after it had opened, neither in the tap, so on an iPhone every set filmed more than a minute after the last one asked again; and on Nov 21 2026 that there were still too many Allow pop-ups when recording (a camera one and a microphone one, and the Crew camera asked from an effect after it opened).
- A set video filmed in the app goes to the phone too, one tap away: iOS never puts a web page's (or the iPhone app's web view's) recording in Photos, and the only way there is the share sheet's Save Video, opened from a tap, so the app offers it (set-videos/phone-copy.ts, set-video-phone-copy.tsx). Which clips were filmed here is read from the picked file's name (`looksFilmedInApp`: a pick from Photos keeps the phone's own name, IMG_1234.MOV, video-output-…, Android's numbered or dated files; anything else counts as filmed here, since an extra offer costs a tap and a missed one costs the clip), and remembered per login until it's saved (`noteFilmed` / `linkFilmed` from the uploader once it lands / `forgetFilmed`; the clip itself while the app is open, a record in localStorage for 7 days so a reload fetches it back from storage). What's saved is the part that was sent, never the whole recording (Nov 22 2026): the trim cut from the recording without re-encoding (`phoneCopyFor`, uploader.ts, a remux of the selection started as the clip is queued, the recording itself when nothing was trimmed; `holdFilmedFile` keeps it), else after a reload or a phone that can't cut it the stored copy, which is trimmed as sent. It's offered right after filming, once that copy is cut (`offerPhoneCopy`: a toast whose Save hands it to the share sheet), on every clip in the set's sheet (Save to Photos, `useSaveSetVideos` through `useSaveMedia` with `holdSaveFile` so the tap opens the sheet at once), before one is removed ("This video isn't in your Photos" with Save to Photos / Remove anyway / Keep, `data-set-video-only-copy`, only for the athlete's own unsaved clip), and at the end of the workout (`PhoneCopyReminder`: the review, the finished workout's card and the recap, every unsaved clip in one share; never a coach viewing as them, never on a computer). A save that reached the share sheet or downloaded (`onSaved`) stops every offer for it — why: on Nov 12 2026 Fionna filmed her hip thrust set in the app and removed it from the set thinking it was on her phone; it wasn't (the file stayed in storage, since `set_video_remove` is a soft delete); and on Nov 22 2026 the coach trimmed his sets and found the whole untrimmed recording in Photos, since Save handed over the clip as filmed.
- A finished workout's set videos are its film: the session's Crew post (the same one slot as its finish post), a clip per slide in the order trained, each slide with its own caption on it (`SlideCaption` in post-card: the exercise, then the set by its name in the workout and what was lifted, "Top set · 145 kg × 5 @ RPE 8", "Backoff 2 · …", "Set 3 · …" (`set_name` on the slide), on a scrim at the bottom (the card feed and a post's page; on a post's own screen it's in the words instead, `data-page-set`), clear of the sound button and dots, weights left out on a post that hides them, never for the athlete themselves or on a coach-only post; read from `community_posts.film` by path in `postSlides`, workout-film.ts, emitted by `community_post_json`), and, when they haven't written one, the post's own caption is the database's `film_caption` of its slides: the best (heaviest, `kg`) set of each main lift filmed (`main`), at most three, else of the first two exercises, "High Bar Squat 365 lb × 6 · 1ct Paused Bench 255 lb × 4", names only on a post that hides weights (`community_posts_film_caption_weights` swaps it when that's switched), no emoji, kept in step as clips are added or moved while it's still the film's own (`filmCaptionFor`; their own words are never touched). Every set is named by one function, `pl_day_set_names` (20270311090000_film_set_names.sql; the server's only), for the film, the coach chat and a move alike: a row the coach calls a backoff (its purpose label, or notes that start "Backoff") is one and what comes before it of the same exercise is the top set; with nothing said, a 1-set row followed by one more row of the same lift is a top set and its backoffs unless the second was logged heavier; numbers run across the rows of one exercise ("Top set" / "Top set 2", "Backoff" / "Backoff 3", else "Set N"), so the same exercise over two rows never says "Set 1" twice. Only the server builds it (`postWorkoutFilmFn`, workout-film.functions.ts): the workout's own login only (never a coach viewing as them), its own clips on logged sets, copied from the coach chat's storage into their community folder (`<user>/film/<completion>/<set video>`; the chat's file never changes) and recorded in `media_video_checks` from the set video's own check, so the post's video guard holds each to a workout post's 90 s / 50 MB (`pickFilmClips`: longer ones stay in the chat only, ten slides at most, photos they added stay first). Each slide is made from the set video's 720p feed copy when it has one (`filmFile`, workout-film.ts; else the original): a set video that went to the coach as it was (an iPhone's 4K original, which its encoder can't shrink in time, `encodeTooSlow`) gets one once the coach has it (`feedCopyPlan`, set-videos/feed-copy.ts: the same selection at 720p for a workout post, never falling back part way, kept only when at most `FEED_COPY_MAX_SHARE` of what was sent), made by the uploader only while their film goes to Crew and nothing for the coach is sending (the coach's clips always go first; a copy still encoding after `FEED_COPY_MAX_MS` is dropped), uploaded next to the original (`<client>/set-videos/<job>-feed.<ext>`), checked as a workout post's video and recorded by `set_video_feed_copy` (20270309090000_set_video_feed_copy.sql: the sender only, that name only, 90 s / 50 MB; it returns the finished workout whose film is already in the feed, which the uploader then brings up to date); a copy gets its own film path (`filmPaths` adds `-feed`), so a post made from the original reads as behind and swaps it in, and the film files the post no longer shows are removed. A copy that can't be made changes nothing, and the coach chat's clip never changes. The copy is made on a server first (Nov 18 2026, 20270410090000_server_feed_copies.sql, set-videos/server-copy.ts pure and tested, server-copy.server.ts): every set video that needs one (`set_video_needs_server_copy` = `needsServerCopy`: a set, not a warm-up, 0.5–91.5 s, 4 MB+, and over 1300 px on its long side or 44 MB) of an athlete whose film goes to Crew (`auto_post_film`, or a film Shared to Crew by hand, `set_video_feed_job_queue` `_force` from `buildWorkoutFilm`, workout-film.server.ts) gets a job (`set_video_feed_jobs`, server only) the moment it lands, and the `feed-copies-tick` hook (woken by the insert and pg_cron every minute while one is due) hands Mux a 2-hour signed link (`muxAssetBody`: a 720p MP4, basic encoding; Mux's older capped-1080p form once if refused), waits for it (`readRendition`, polling within the request up to `SERVER_COPY_WAIT_MS`, then each tick), downloads it, keeps it only when it fits a workout post and is lighter than what was sent (`copyKeeps`), stores it as `<client>/set-videos/<job>-feed.mp4`, checks it like any upload (`media_video_checks`, owner the sender), records it (`set_video_feed_copy_server`, service role only), brings a film already in the feed up to date (`buildWorkoutFilm` as the server, the athlete's own `auto_post_film`) and deletes the asset from Mux; a failed try backs off (`afterFailure`, `SERVER_COPY_TRIES`) and then it's the phone's. With no keys on the server (MUX_TOKEN_ID / MUX_TOKEN_SECRET) nothing is sent anywhere: jobs wait an hour and phones keep making copies; once a job runs with the keys `app_settings` 'feed_copies' says so (`set_video_server_copies`, `serverCopiesOn`, server-copy-flag.ts) and phones make none for a new clip, the catch-up taking only a clip still without one after `SERVER_COPY_GRACE_MS` (30 min), and the film row says it's coming without asking to keep the app open. Otherwise a clip the film leaves out for its size with no copy (sent before copies existed, or whose copy never got made) gets one the next time the athlete's own app is open (`catchUpFeedCopies`, catch-up.ts, started by `SetVideoResume` 20 s after a launch, 5 s after the app comes back on screen, and when the workout's film has a clip waiting on its copy (`askFeedCopyCatchUp` → `FEED_COPY_CATCH_UP_EVENT`): one small read of their own clips from the last `CATCH_UP_DAYS` over 50 MB and no longer than 90 s, each fetched back from storage only when its turn comes, `loadSentClip`, and copied the same way; one try a clip an app open (`takeCatchUpLaunch`), at most `CATCH_UP_LAUNCHES` of those on a phone, and a phone that found nothing looks again after `CATCH_UP_EVERY_MS` (15 min), at once after it sends a clip that needs a copy (`clearCatchUpChecked`)), and the film post picks it up. Who sees it is one choice saved on the account, Crew or Coach only (`community_profiles.auto_post_film`: true = Crew, the default; `FilmAudienceOptions`, film-audience-options.tsx, a neutral two-way control: "Who sees your film" at the end of the review, `FilmAudienceChoice`, and "Who sees my film" in Crew settings, `PostMyFilmRow`; hooks in workout-film-pref.ts so settings never load the logger), and both say the coach gets every clip either way: each set video is already its own message in the coach chat, so Coach only posts nothing anywhere (a coach-only feed post would repeat the chat, and would replace a Pulse story). It posts by itself (`auto`) only while the choice is Crew, every clip has landed, the review is in (the choice lives there) or it finished more than `FILM_REVIEW_WAIT_MS` ago, and they haven't taken the session's post out of Crew (hidden, coach only or archived stays that way); a clip that lands later is added the next time the workout is open. Coach only, the finished workout's card and its recap show the film (`WorkoutFilmRow`: posters, "3 sets on film", In the feed · View, Coach only · Share to Crew, and what's on film but not in the post with why, `filmNotes` / `filmLeftOutStatus`, workout-film.ts: "1 more is getting ready for Crew. Keep the app open a minute or two." for a clip waiting on its copy (`preparing`, read again every 30 s while it waits), over 90 seconds, too big on a phone that can't make a copy, past the ten a post holds; never "over 90 seconds" for a clip that's only big) and Share to Crew posts it any time — why: the coach wants clients sharing their lifts, and a post made for them from the clip and the set's own numbers is the easiest share there is, while a client who'd rather not must never have a set posted without saying so; and 8 of the first 9 set videos went up as 4K originals (~50 MB for 30 s, the encoder giving up after 4 s), five times what a feed clip needs, so film posts were slow to load on a swipe and about half of those clips were over a workout post's 50 MB and never made the film at all; and Jared's Nov 12 film showed 4 of his 9 sets, the other 5 being 4K originals of 52–70 MB sent hours before copies existed; and on Nov 13 2026 Jared's paused bench top set and first backoff both read "Set 1" (two rows of the same exercise, each counting from 1), with a 🎥 on every caption and the post's own caption only repeating the workout's name ("Thursday — Secondary Squat + Primary Bench · 9 sets on film 🎥"); and on Nov 18 2026 Jared's Monday film showed 5 of his 6 sets (the coach then asked for the copy made on a server, so no clip waits on the athlete's phone): his Pull Up (58.5 MB, 31 s) went to the coach as it was, the app was left before its copy was made, the phone that had looked earlier and found nothing waited up to 6 hours (and only for a fresh launch, which a home-screen app rarely gets) to look again, and the workout said the clip was "over 90 seconds".
- A set video's bar path and bar speed are read on the device that has the clip, never by a server or a model (Nov 19 2026, rebuilt Nov 20 2026, detection-first and both-way tracking Nov 21 2026: bar-path/3; src/lib/lift-analysis/, pure and tested except run.ts / auto.ts / queries.ts; 20270422090000_set_video_analyses.sql, 20270427093000_video_velocity_logging.sql): the plate on the end of the bar is the only thing followed and the only ruler. The flow is Find → Confirm → Analyze → Results (`LiftAnalysisView`, one main button each). Find (detect.ts `proposePlate`, find.ts): four frames a second over the clip (12–40, `sampleCount`), every circle by gradient Hough with a vote window that grows with the radius (`circleCandidates`), circles linked into tracks by what a bar can travel, rim coverage and size (`linkTracks`), the plate the biggest well-seen circle that moves up and down; a circle that only ever sits in one or two places (two plates on a tree read as one, `fixedSpots`), one that never passes through the heights in between when one that does is there (`passesThrough`), and one whose picture never changes (`motionEnergy`: under `STILL_PICTURE` of the most-changing track or `STILL_OVER_FLOOR` × a circle that never moved) are still plates, never the bar's; a "circle" drawn through the lifter and the plate gives way to the plate (`ENCLOSED_PLATE`, `roundness`). Its size is the plate's outer edge (rim-outer.ts `outerRim`), never the lip or a smaller plate in front: the centre first by radial symmetry (`symmetryCentre`: every edge votes along its own normal, an oval's normals for each shape tried, `SYMMETRY_ASPECTS`, so the vote never depends on where the guess started), then each ray's clear edges (down to `REL_EDGE` of its strongest, a black plate's edge in front of a black rack included), the oval shape the edges line up on best (`ASPECTS`, judged at `AGREE_TIGHT`), the outermost well-supported oval round the plate's own centre (`CONCENTRIC_SHARE`: a head or shoulder peeking out behind the plate is a circle round its own centre), refitted against the oval and with the centre fixed (`fitAxes`). The proposal is high (a confident track with a clear edge: read by itself after the video is sent, auto.ts), medium (shown preselected, never read alone: `needs_confirm`, kept as a `needs_tap` row whose `suggestions` lead with the ring, `preselect` + `fa` / `fb`, suggestions.ts, so opening it is one look and Analyze) or low (no ring it can vouch for). Confirm (`ConfirmPanel`, `BarPathPlayer` `marker`) shows one ring, never a choice of circles (Nov 22 2026: `oneRing`, suggestions.ts: the proposal's key, else the best circle seen snapped to the plate's rim, `snapRing` → `locatePlate`; every stored `needs_tap` row keeps its best guess whatever the doubt, `proposalSuggestions(res.proposal)`, and one stored before that with no `preselect` is found again on open; the other circles are never drawn, only snapped to by a tap): the video paused on the ring; a finger on the ring drags it with the picture under it magnified above the finger (`drawLoupe`), the dot on its edge sizes it, a tap anywhere snaps it to the plate there (`locatePlate`: one frame decoded, `plateAtTap`) or, with no plate there, leaves it for the person to size; the biggest plate's diameter is one row, 450 mm (standard, unconfirmed) unless 450 / 400 / 325 mm or a typed size is picked, never guessed from the plate's look; what about the footage costs accuracy is said there with how to film it better (footage.ts `footageIssues`: under 50 fps, a plate under `SMALL_PLATE_PX` in the camera's own pixels, the plate leaving the frame, a dark picture, an oval plate), never blocking; How to film it is one inline panel (`FilmingGuide`, the lift's own picture of what the phone sees and where it goes, five lines). Analyze (run.ts `analyzeVideo`, engine.ts `runEngine` for frames in memory): every frame followed forward and backward from each key (keyed.ts: a key is a frame where the centre and size are known, the confirmed ring or a fix; `KeyedPass`, the backward pass decoded in `BACK_CHUNK_S` chunks), by the plate's own picture (template.ts, follow.ts) held strictly to the key's size (`RESIZE_TOLERANCE`) and to what a loaded bar can do between frames (`MAX_BAR_SPEED_MM_S`); where the two directions agree within `AGREE_SHARE` of a radius they're averaged, where they don't the unbroken chain wins, and where both are unbroken and still apart the frame is lost (`mergePasses`), never chosen between or guessed; lost stretches inside the set are kept on the read (`track.lost`) with the keys (`track.keys`). Then the plate's size at full resolution in still frames (`MEASURE_LONG_SIDE`, `calibrationTimes`), the most complete rims counting (`rimSize`: share within 0.05 of the best first), and, when rims at both ends of the travel say the plate is bigger at one end (a phone tilted up or down: `sizeGradient`, 1–15 % across the travel), heights are remapped so one scale fits all of them (analyze.ts `level`). Reps (kinematics.ts `analyzeReps`): which way a rep goes is the exercise's (`repDirection`), else where the bar rests (`restingDirection`); the concentric is the unbroken run around its peak velocity (`mainPhase`), its boundaries from a slice of the same time at any frame rate (`BOUNDARY_SLICE_S`: a narrower one at 60 fps read the same rep ~5 % faster), the start / stop thresholds from the measured scatter (`sigmaMove`) and the margins from it with a floor of an eighth of a pixel (`MARGIN_FLOOR_PX`); a rise counts only after a full descent (lowered first) and at least 0.55 of the set's typical rise, a rise that falls back short of lockout is a missed rep, and a rep's mean velocity needs both its ends seen (`endSeen`). Every number is a `Metric` with its 95 % margin and a tier (measured ≤ 8 %, estimated ≤ 25 % with ≈, else unavailable, never shown); a sticking point is shown only when a dip could have been seen (the peak read, 50 fps or more). The set's velocity (verdict.ts `velocityVerdict`) is the plain mean of every rep's MCV and only when every rep was found, complete and measured, the plate followed in `ACCEPT_RULES.minTracked` (85 %) of the clip, the scale known to 3 %, the camera still and near side-on, 24 fps or more and no rep's range a clear outlier; otherwise nothing is logged and the reasons are said in words. The server puts it on the set (`_apply_video_velocity`) only when the read passed, the set's logged rep count equals the reps the video saw, the plate's size was set or the load means full-size plates (`loadMeansFullPlates`), and never over a velocity typed in (`velocity_source` device / video); a moved, removed or changed read takes its number back. Results lead with the set's average and whether it's on the set (`loggedState`), then each rep (its phases in the lift's own words, `PHASE_WORDS`), the notes with Fix tracking on a lost plate, and More detail (rep to rep, load–velocity, How it's measured) folded. Fix tracking (`FixPanel`): the scrubber marks the lost stretches in amber, the ring follows the read path and shows dashed where the plate was lost, a drag puts it right on that frame (a correction), and Analyze again reads from the same keys plus the corrections (`ShownSeed` `keys`), never from scratch; ⋯ Start over finds the bar again. Every outcome is kept (`pl_set_video_analyses.status`), so the line under the clip says where it stands (`overallProgress`, progress.ts, which the line imports without the engine) and no phone reads a video twice; a read the app closing cut off is read again on the next open (`resumeLiftAnalyses`); a read an older engine refused or left "needs a look" is read again by the current one (`rereadPlan`, reread.ts; `queueLiftReread`, auto.ts: on the athlete's next open for their set videos of the last 14 days, two an open, and in the background whenever anyone opens one, its old results on screen meanwhile), from the ring the athlete confirmed when there was one (`source` 'tap': its keys, else its plate, and a plate size they set), else found again by itself; the new read replaces the old only when it's a read (or the old was only "needs a look"), and a read whose speed is on the set is never touched.
